限制紧扣识别和机制：无法区分 technology anxiety 与 privacy concern；样本只包含自选择使用 self-checkout 的顾客。未来研究方向因此自然指向 survey/interview 和更完整 checkout choice 数据。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- tension 标题：[Improving convenience] or [saving face]? An empirical analysis of [technology] in [context].
- 现实悖论：Although [technology] can be more convenient, it is still not consistently used by many customers.
- 场景差异：A key difference between [technology A] and [technology B] is that [A] must be used in public settings where [others may watch].
- 机制猜想：We conjecture that [users] do not use [technology] simply because of [social mechanism].
- 数据环境：The use of [same provider/platform] creates a controlled environment for examining [choice].
- 选择偏差回应：To tackle potential self-selection, we estimate the model using progressively restricted samples.
- 经济量级过渡：Although our results are statistically significant, it is also important to show that the effects are economically significant.
- 设计启示：Firms could increase usage by modeling these factors into the design, operation, and promotion of [technology].

## P0115：Salesforce Incentives, Market Information, and Production/Inventory Planning

### Title / Abstract

标题用三个并列名词直接标出交叉点：Salesforce Incentives、Market Information、Production/Inventory Planning。读者一眼知道本文不是纯销售激励，也不是纯库存计划，而是两者的接口。

摘要开头的 “Salespeople are the eyes and ears of the firms they serve” 很有记忆点。它先用现实角色定位，再进入 truthful disclosure and hard work 的双目标冲突，最后说明比较 Gonik scheme 和 menu of linear contracts。

### Introduction

Introduction 的结构是现实角色 -> 双目标冲突 -> 经典实践方案 -> 标准理论方案 -> 本文比较。它没有一上来写 principal-agent model，而是先让读者理解为什么销售预测和努力激励会冲突。

可学习的是把实践机制 Gonik 放在前面，用真实管理故事吸引读者，再用 agency theory 给出学术比较对象。

### Literature Review

文献综述分三层：salesforce compensation、moral hazard/adverse selection agency theory、marketing-operations interface。每一层都说明本文如何推进，而不是只列名字。

### Model / Method Section

模型段先搭一般 pooling/separating 结构，再分别进入 first best、single linear、menu linear、Gonik。复杂合同比较要先给一个总框架，否则读者会迷路。

作者特别强调 agent private information benefits the firm in two ways：measurement of effort 和 production/inventory matching。这个句子把模型贡献压得很准。

### Results / Propositions

结果顺序是 benchmark -> closed-form solution -> algorithm -> numerical comparison。理论文若某部分不能闭式求解，可以先给结构定理和界，再用算法与数值比较补足。

数值结果解释不是只报谁大谁小，而是回到机制：Gonik 不能诱导不同市场条件下不同努力，因此被 menu linear 支配。

### Managerial Implications

管理含义不是“别用 Gonik”，而是“检查薪酬机制是否同时完成信息揭示、努力激励和运营计划改善”。这是把数学比较翻成管理动作的关键。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论回到 Gonik 原文三目标，并讨论修改 Gonik 的可能性。这个写法既尊重经典实践方案，又清楚说明本文发现的边界。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 现实角色开场：[Agents] are the eyes and ears of the firms they serve.
- 双目标冲突：Inducing [truthful information revelation] and providing incentives for [effort] can sometimes be conflicting goals.
- 经典机制进入：Perhaps the most well-known solution is due to [classic practitioner/scholar].
- 比较目标：The main purpose of this paper is to compare [practice-based mechanism] with [theory-based mechanism].
- 信息用途升级：The agent's private information benefits the principal in two ways: [measurement] and [operational planning].
- 机制短板：This appears to be due to [mechanism]'s inability to induce different effort levels for different market conditions.
- 克制结论：The scheme is clever and useful, but it is dominated by [alternative] in the model considered here.

## P0117：Coordination Mechanisms for a Distribution System with One Supplier and Multiple Retailers

### Title / Abstract

标题采用“机制目标 + 场景结构”的朴素顶刊写法：Coordination Mechanisms 对应研究目标，one supplier and multiple retailers 对应模型边界。它不追求花哨，而是让读者立刻知道这是一篇供应链协调机制论文。

摘要的推进顺序很标准：先定义系统，再说 centralized optimum，再给负结论“传统订单量折扣不够”，最后给正机制“三个折扣组件 + 固定费”。这种写法适合机制设计论文，因为读者最关心旧工具为什么失败、新工具具体补了什么。

### Introduction

Introduction 从现实折扣实践切入：现实中的折扣并不只基于单次订单量，还常常基于 annual volume、order size 和 order frequency。这个观察让模型复杂性显得自然，而不是作者硬加机制。

可学习的动作是：先把“理论常用工具”和“现实管理工具”放在一起比较。一旦二者不一致，research question 就自然出现：现实为什么需要更复杂的工具，旧理论工具漏掉了什么。

### Literature Review

文献综述不是大面积铺供应链协调文献，而是紧扣 quantity discount 是否足以协调。它把自己放在一个窄而重要的问题上：传统折扣机制在多零售商非同质系统里是否仍然有效。

### Model / Method Section

模型段的写作顺序值得模仿：先写 centralized benchmark，明确“什么叫协调”；再证明旧机制不能实现这个 benchmark；最后构造新机制。这个顺序比直接提出复杂合同更有说服力，因为读者先看到缺口，再接受复杂性。

### Results / Propositions

Theorem 1 和 Theorem 2 用 counterexample 建立负结论，Theorem 3 用 constructive proof 给出正结论。负结论不需要把所有情况都解完，只要证明“不能保证”即可；正结论则必须把机制如何复制集中式目标讲清楚。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示直接对应合同设计动作：年销量、订单批量、订货频率和固定费分别承担不同功能。这种写法比“企业应加强协调”更像顶刊，因为它告诉管理者应该改哪个合同条款。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论用 alternative holding-cost model 做扩展，说明核心机制不是依赖单一持有成本假设。这是一种克制的稳健性写法：不大幅扩展场景，但回应最直接的模型边界。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 实践悖论：In practice, [contract/payment schemes] are often based on multiple criteria, whereas most analytical models focus on [single criterion].
- 研究问题压缩：This raises the question of whether [traditional mechanism] is sufficient to coordinate [specific system].
- 负结论表达：We show that no traditional [mechanism] based solely on [dimension] suffices to achieve [coordination goal].
- 机制拆分：The proposed scheme consists of components based on [annual volume], [order quantity], and [order frequency].
- 固定费定位：Fixed fees are used to allocate surplus and do not affect operational decisions.
- 复杂性辩护：The additional components are needed because each dimension captures a distinct externality in the system.
- 机制设计论文顺序：Define the centralized benchmark -> show the old instrument is insufficient -> construct a new instrument -> compare with uncoordinated benchmarks.

## P0191：Pricing with Cookies: Behavior-Based Price Discrimination and Spatial Competition

### Title / Abstract

标题前半句 “Pricing with Cookies” 有现实钩子，后半句 “Behavior-Based Price Discrimination and Spatial Competition” 给学术分类。这个写法适合 IO/平台论文：让读者先看见现象，再看见理论归属。

摘要按“模型设定 -> 信息结构 -> 研究对象 -> 固定位置结果 -> 内生位置结果 -> 管理反直觉”推进。它没有把所有公式写进去，而是把最应记住的两个机制放出来：非对称均衡和双方利润下降。

### Introduction

Introduction 的动作链是：big data/cookie/会员卡现实趋势 -> 个性化定价可能增利也可能引发隐私公平担忧 -> 旧文献认为消费者信息加剧竞争 -> 旧文献多用对称信息 -> 现实 cookie 信息是非对称的。这个 gap 很小但很硬，因为它直接改变均衡结构。

可学写法：不要只说“已有文献忽略 X”，要说明“忽略 X 会改变哪个核心结论”。本文的说法就是：把对称信息改成非对称个体信息后，唯一对称均衡变成多个非对称均衡。

### Literature Review

文献综述按模型维度分类：static/dynamic、third-degree/personalized、profit hurting/profit improving。这样的分类比按年份罗列更有力量，因为读者能看到本文精确嵌入哪个矩阵格子。

### Model / Method Section

模型假设都有现实解释。尤其是第二期先统一 poaching price、再 personalized price 的 timing，作者解释为 regular price 是更高层、更慢的管理决策，而个性化价格调整更快。这个动作提醒我们：技术性 timing 假设最好给现实管理理由。

### Results / Propositions

Lemma 1 后用图解释非对称均衡的直觉：谁先抢到大市场份额，谁第二期更能保护地盘；但另一方也能通过挖客价获得补偿。顶刊写法不是“公式结束”，而是“公式后立刻把机制讲成人话”。

结果顺序也很自然：先给均衡存在和形状，再比较企业/消费者/福利，再和最近 benchmark FT 比较，最后扩展到一边信息和内生位置。每个 section 都回答读者的一个自然追问。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示用 Tesco 和 Asda，但作者保持克制：它是模型含义的现实解释，不是因果检验。理论论文可以用真实案例增强读者理解，但要避免把 anecdote 写成 evidence。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论把未来研究写得很具体：战略性删除 cookie、延迟购买、公平关切。每个拓展都说明可能通过什么机制改变结论，例如删除 cookie 会让挖客低价也暴露给老顾客，从而提高 poaching 成本。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 现实技术进入：Firms can target [pricing/marketing/product characteristics] using [customer-level information].
- 争议转入：The ability to use [data] has spurred research, but it has also led to concerns about [privacy/equity/fairness].
- 旧文献承接：Existing research shows that access to [information] can intensify competition and hurt profitability.
- 关键假设缺口：A key assumption in these studies is that competing firms acquire symmetric information.
- 现实修正：In many applications, however, information collection is asymmetric.
- 强贡献表达：This is in sharp contrast to studies that obtain a unique symmetric equilibrium.
- 机制句：The information advantage over own customers allows firms to protect their turf better.
- 管理主句：More customer information is bad for competing firms insofar as the sole use of customer information is for pricing.

## P0264：Entry of Copycats of Luxury Brands

### Title / Abstract

标题短而场景清楚：Entry of Copycats of Luxury Brands。摘要第一句立刻补出模型创新：two product attributes 和 two consumer utilities。这个组合告诉读者，本文不是泛泛谈山寨，而是把山寨属性和消费者效用同时拆开。

摘要发现顺序很好：先说什么山寨能进，再说为什么高质量反而可能进不去，最后说福利反转。三个 finding 都围绕同一条主线：copycat 的质量和相似度如何改变进入与福利。

### Introduction

Introduction 用 Prada CEO 的引语开头，制造“山寨到底坏不坏”的 tension。然后列出山寨五个现实特征：high resemblance、low price、nondeceptive、low quality、rapid launch。这个特征列表直接生成模型变量，是非常值得学的写法。

### Literature Review

文献综述不只是说已有文献研究 counterfeit，而是指出本文不同：nondeceptive copycats、physical resemblance、quality、consumption utility、status utility。这是“逐个变量定位 gap”的写法。

### Model / Method Section

模型段先解释 $\alpha$ 和 $q$ 的现实含义，再解释为什么 $q<1$ 是必要的。作者把看似简单的假设讲成“为了让 copycat 仍然依赖 incumbent 的品牌价值”。这让假设服务于 research question。

### Results / Propositions

命题后立刻翻译成管理动作。Proposition 1 的条件里，$\lambda$ 对应广告和名人代言，$\alpha$ 对应防复制设计，$q$ 对应山寨质量。读者不需要自己从公式猜含义。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示嵌入命题解释中，而不是单独泛泛写。正品品牌可以降低价格覆盖市场、提高身份价值、降低可复制性；监管者要关注低质量山寨的身份稀释和质量损失。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论先回到最重要的现实解释：为什么多数山寨高相似低质量。限制部分逐项对应关键假设：上市时间、身份效用设定、正品是否推出低端产品、山寨买家的失面子效应。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 引语制造 tension：[Industry quote] suggests that [phenomenon] may not be entirely undesirable.
- 现实特征列表：In general, most [products] exhibit the following characteristics: [1], [2], [3].
- 问题推出：These observations have motivated us to examine the following research questions.
- 双维度模型：Our model incorporates [product attributes] and [consumer utilities].
- 反常识句：By contrast to conventional wisdom, we find that [phenomenon] does not always improve [outcome].
- 参数管理化：The incumbent can influence [parameter] through [advertising/design/commitment].
- 非单调结果：Better [attribute] does not always generate higher profit because it triggers more intense competition.

## P0978：Competing with Copycats When Customers Are Strategic

### Title / Abstract

标题把三方关系压得很清楚：Competing with Copycats When Customers Are Strategic。它直接说明本文不是一般山寨竞争，而是“山寨 + 战略消费者”。

摘要按主体决策推进：制造商广告和两期价格，copycat 进入和定价，消费者是否买、何时买、买谁。最后列三个反直觉结果，覆盖制造商质量、buy-now mentality 和战略消费者对 copycat 的影响。

### Introduction

Introduction 先用 “imitation is the sincerest form of flattery” 的俗语反转制造 tension，再给 Monoprice、Xiaomi、Zara、Forever21 等现实案例。它很会把普通读者熟悉的商业现象转成研究问题：消费者甚至会等山寨出现。

### Literature Review

文献综述把 entry deterrence、strategic consumers、quick response 和 copycat 文献连起来。每一块都说明本文多了什么：广告外溢、消费者等待和山寨进入共同决定制造商运营策略。

### Model / Method Section

模型段清楚区分战略消费者和短视消费者。战略消费者比较第一期效用和第二期最佳选择；短视消费者只看当前选项。这个对比让行为机制非常透明。

### Results / Propositions

Proposition 1 的三区域框架很值得学：先给一个总分类，再让后续所有比较静态回到区域切换。这样复杂模型不会散。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示落到具体动作：低/中质量产品可降价和少广告威慑，高质量产品不宜价格战，应考虑法律行动；快反供应链不是无条件好，要看山寨速度和消费者耐心。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论给出 testable hypotheses，把理论模型结果转成未来实证问题。这一点很适合后续 skill：理论论文也可以主动为实证研究铺路。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 俗语反转：The old adage [quote] does not necessarily imply that [phenomenon] is welcome.
- 现实痛点：Firms spend scarce capital to [develop/market] new products only to find [imitators] flooding the market.
- 消费者等待：Some consumers now wait for [alternative] to appear, actively seeking it out as a substitute.
- 搭便车机制：Advertising increases the size of the pie, but the firm may end up sharing the benefits with [entrant].
- 质量反噬：The manufacturer may be a victim of its own success.
- 运营边界：The firm needs a deep understanding of its competitive environment before investing in [capability].
- 理论转实证：The managerial implications and testable hypotheses are summarized as follows.

## P0106：Auctioning Supply Contracts

### Title / Abstract

标题极短但有动作感：Auctioning Supply Contracts。它没有写成泛泛的 procurement auctions，而是把熟悉的 supply contracts 变成被 auction 的对象，标题本身就展示机制创新。

摘要按 problem -> mechanism -> implementation -> application -> comparison 写。先说明 purchase quantity 和 price 都要决定，再给出“buyer first designs a supply contract and then invites suppliers to bid”的机制顺序，随后连接 slotting allowances、VMI、returns contracts、revenue-sharing contracts 和 fixed-quantity auction。

### Introduction

引言用“双文献缺口”制造 tension：OM procurement models often treat prices as exogenous；economics procurement models focus on price discovery while fixing quantity decisions。然后用一句目的句收束：understand how price discovery can be integrated with quantity decisions。

可复用动作：不是说“少有人研究 X”，而是精确指出两个成熟文献分别固定了哪个变量，再说明现实管理者必须联合决定这两个变量。

### Literature Review

文献综述按研究流派组织：procurement auctions、supply chain coordination、auction theory in OM、incentive contracts in auctions。每一类都说明本文的区别：本文把 supply contract 和 auction mechanism 结合以获得 optimal procurement strategy。

### Model / Method Section

模型写法先一般后具体。第 2 节用一般收益函数 $R(Q)$ 建机制，第 3 节再放入 newsvendor 场景解释现实制度。这种结构让文章既有理论一般性，又有管理直觉。

公式后的解释很值得学：virtual cost 后解释 information rent；supply contract auction 后解释 business opportunity；fixed-quantity auction 后解释 informational loss。模型论文不要只给定理，要把每个数学对象变成管理语言。

### Results / Propositions

Theorem 的顺序有层次：先证明旧机制 quantity auction 最优，再指出它的 auction format 有限制，最后推出更好解释实践的 supply contract auction。结果不是平铺，而是一步步把读者带向作者真正想推的机制。

### Managerial Implications

管理含义通过现实制度映射写出来：up-front fee 对应 slotting allowance；delegated quantity decision 对应 VMI；decreasing average payment 对应 quantity discount；demand-dependent payment 对应 returns/revenue-sharing contracts。可学习的是把抽象机制逐项翻译成管理制度。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论先复述“supply contract embedded in auction mechanism”这条主线，再承认 fixed-quantity auction simpler but suboptimal，最后列未来扩展：increasing marginal costs、economies of scale、asymmetric/risk-averse suppliers、supply-curve bids、audits。未来研究不是套话，而是直接对应模型被简化掉的关键假设。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- 双文献缺口：Literature A models [decision X] but treats [decision Y] as exogenous; literature B studies [decision Y] but fixes [decision X].
- 现实联合决策：In reality, managers often need to determine [X] and discover [Y] jointly.
- 机制顺序：An optimal strategy requires the buyer first to [commit/design] and then to [invite/auction].
- 制度映射：This strategy establishes a connection between [mechanism] and [common practice].
- 信息租金解释：The expression indicates that the lack of information is reflected in [virtual cost / rent].
- 简单机制对照：Although suboptimal, this strategy is simpler because [specific operational reason].
- 未来研究写法：It would be interesting to see whether [mechanism] survives when [specific assumption] is relaxed.

## P0108：Managing Downstream Competition via Capacity Allocation

### Title / Abstract

标题用 “via” 把管理目标和工具连接起来：Managing Downstream Competition via Capacity Allocation。可学习这种标题结构：Managing [strategic outcome] via [operational rule]。

摘要先给 supply chain 场景，再给 limited capacity 和 Cournot competition，最后比较 proportional 与 lexicographic，直接点出 lexicographic dampens competition and increases profits。

### Introduction

引言从现实常识进入：capacity investment is long-term，short-run mismatch is inevitable，therefore rationing is necessary。然后把研究问题写成 allocation mechanism influences strategic behavior and profits。

最值得学的是作者先给机制直觉：高优先级零售商订满产能以 keep other retailers out of the game。模型还没开始，读者已经知道结果为什么可能成立。

### Literature Review

文献综述通过对比已有 independent markets 设定来建立贡献。已有研究中供应商可能偏好 proportional；本文加入 market-level Cournot competition 后，偏好反转为 lexicographic。可学习动作：用一个关键现实维度改变已有机制排序。

### Model / Method Section

变量链条很清楚：order $m_i$ -> allocation $g_i(m)$ -> market price $M-\sum g_i$ -> retailer profit -> supplier sales。写模型时每个变量都要能讲出现实含义和它如何进入下一环。

### Results / Propositions

结果写法是 Lemma best response -> Theorem equilibrium -> intuition paragraph。尤其在分段均衡论文中，直觉段比公式阈值更重要。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示有一个关键限制：优先级必须独立于当前订单量，否则机制会退化为 proportional allocation。这类限制条件应写进 managerial implication，避免夸大结论。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论把未来研究具体对应到模型假设：non-identical retailers、price competition、capacity/market uncertainty、dynamic turn-and-earn。不是泛泛“future research can relax assumptions”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Managing [outcome] via [operational mechanism].
- In the short run, mismatches between [capacity] and [demand] are inevitable.
- The mechanism used to allocate [scarce resource] influences [strategic behavior].
- To keep [rivals] out of the game, [player] chooses [preemptive action].
- Market-level competition can alter [mechanism preference].
- The advantage is derived from the fact that [priority/status] cannot be influenced by [current strategic choice].

## P0062：Information Distortion in a Supply Chain: The Bullwhip Effect

### Title / Abstract

标题用冒号把学术机制和现象标签合并：Information Distortion in a Supply Chain: The Bullwhip Effect。前半句解释本质，后半句命名现象。

摘要不从模型开始，而从供应链订单信息的扭曲开始，随后定义 bullwhip effect，再列四个来源和治理方向。适合学习“经典现象型论文”摘要写法。

### Introduction

引言先把 information flows 定位为 supply chain coordination 的关键机制，再用真实公司案例说明订单与销售不一致。它不是抽象说“信息很重要”，而是展示订单数据如何误导生产、库存和运输。

### Literature Review

文献综述的核心对比是：Forrester 强调系统动态，Sterman 强调 misperceptions of feedback，本文强调 rational optimizing behavior and institutional structure。可学习的是用行为假设差异建立贡献。

### Model / Method Section

方法结构很经典：先给 idealized situation，再逐一 relax four conditions。每个放松对应一个机制：demand signal processing、rationing game、order batching、price variations。

### Results / Propositions

定理服务于机制解释，而不是炫技。Theorem 1 解释预测更新，Theorem 2 解释短缺抢货，Theorem 3 解释批量节奏，Theorem 4-5 解释促销囤货。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示用表格连接 causes、contributing factors、counter-measures、state of practice。可学习动作：现象型论文最终必须变成诊断表和行动表。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结尾承认数据共享激励没有解决：制造商需要零售商数据，但零售商为何分享、收益如何分配仍是开放问题。这种边界陈述很有学术克制感。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- [Term] refers to the phenomenon where [observable pattern].
- We propose four sources of [phenomenon]: [A], [B], [C], and [D].
- Unlike [prior work], we develop simple mathematical models that capture [institutional structure] and [optimizing behavior].
- Identification of the causes leads to prescriptions for alleviating [problem].
- The paper is silent about the challenging question of why [party] should provide [data/resource].

## P0069：Supply Chain Coordination with Revenue-Sharing Contracts

### Title / Abstract

标题直接用 Strengths and Limitations，显示文章目标是系统评估机制，而不是单向证明机制好。摘要信息密集，但完整覆盖 coordination、profit allocation、contract equivalence、competing retailers、limitations 和 effort extension。

### Introduction

引言用具体数字建立问题：65 dollars per tape、3 dollars per rental、22 rentals break-even，收益共享后批发价降到约 8 dollars，break-even 降到约 6 rentals。顶刊写作里，好的现实动机常常来自这种能直接算出扭曲的数字。

### Literature Review

文章把文献综述嵌入问题推进：每介绍一个已有合同，就说明 revenue sharing 与它等价、不同或更强的地方。可学习动作是合同谱系比较，而不是孤立介绍自己的合同。

### Model / Method Section

模型用一般 $R(q,p)$ 统一租赁、销售、确定需求、随机需求、fixed-price newsvendor 和 price-setting newsvendor。这是“用抽象函数扩大适用面”的好样板。

### Results / Propositions

Theorem 1 是极简强结果：一个条件 $w=\phi c-c_r$ 让零售商利润等于系统利润的 $\phi$ 倍。顶刊模型常需要这种一句话能讲清楚的数学机关。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示始终绑定边界：收益共享适合收入易监控、努力影响小、传统批发合同扭曲大的行业。机制强不等于无条件推荐。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论用 “With so much going for it...” 先承认机制强，再解释为什么不 ubiquitous。这个转折很适合学习：强机制论文也要写现实不普遍的原因。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Under [contract], [party] pays [unit fee] plus a percentage of [realized revenue].
- We demonstrate that [contract] coordinates [system] and arbitrarily allocates [profit].
- [Contract A] is equivalent to [contract B] in the sense that they generate the same cash flows for any realization of [uncertainty].
- Despite its numerous merits, [contract] has limitations that help explain why it is not prevalent in all industries.
- The gains from coordination may not cover the administrative burden of [contract].
- While [contract] coordinates [decision X], it works against coordination of [decision Y].

## P1089：Search, Obfuscation, and Price Elasticities on the Internet

### Title / Abstract

标题用机制名词而不是样本名：Search, Obfuscation, and Price Elasticities on the Internet。它把技术承诺、企业反应和可测结果压在一起。可学习动作：不要把标题写成 “An Empirical Study of [platform]”，而要写成 “[mechanism A], [mechanism B], and [outcome] in [context]”。

摘要的反转结构很强：搜索技术让某些产品需求 extremely price-sensitive；retailers, though, engage in obfuscation，使其他产品没那么敏感。这个 though 是顶刊写法的关键按钮，用来把常识推进成研究问题。

### Introduction

引言从 frictionless commerce 的时代性承诺进入，再马上说 effect is not so clear-cut。它没有否认主流直觉，而是把直觉条件化：搜索降低一部分摩擦，但企业会投资制造另一部分摩擦。

可迁移动作：先承认一个读者熟悉的强命题，再指出现实里出现了反作用力。结构是 “When [phenomenon] first emerged, one heard a lot about [promise]. This paper examines [setting]. A primary observation is that [effect] is not so clear-cut.”

### Literature Review

文献综述短但有功能。每类文献都对应一个本文任务：搜索理论解释 incomplete learning，add-on pricing 解释 adverse selection，在线价格研究说明已有点击/价格分散证据，本文用 actual quantity data 推进。

可迁移动作：文献综述不要堆领域名，而要写“前人有什么数据限制/机制空缺，本文用什么数据或机制补上”。

### Model / Method Section

理论部分主动声明完整模型很复杂，因此只 sketch 两个机制。这个克制很适合混合方法论文：理论不是为了炫技，而是为了生成可检验的 empirical implications。

实证方法部分直接承认 price endogeneity，并解释为什么时间序列特征、flexible trends、cost instruments 和 other-speed instruments 有帮助。高水平写法不是假装没有问题，而是把担忧和处理办法一一配对。

### Results / Propositions

结果段把系数翻译成现实量级：从第一名到第七名低质销量下降 83%，中质下降 66%，高质下降 51%。这种写法比只报 elasticities 更容易让读者记住机制。

作者按机制组织结果：先证明搜索确实有效，再证明 loss leader，再证明 incomplete search，再证明 adverse selection 和 markup。不是按表格顺序流水账。

### Managerial Implications

本文不是商学院期刊，管理启示不显式集中，但机制本身能落到决策：零售商要设计入口品和升级品组合；平台要标准化多维信息；监管者要注意只披露最低价可能不够。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论保持双向克制：互联网 clearly facilitates search，但 also allows firms to adopt obfuscation。未来研究方向非常具体：endogenous quality、search engines with search frictions、individualized prices。这些方向都从正文机制自然延伸。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- When [technology] first emerged, one heard a lot about the promise of [ideal outcome].
- The effect of [technology] on [friction] is not so clear-cut.
- Advances in [consumer tool] are accompanied by investments by firms in [counter-strategy].
- We examine an environment where [mechanism] plays a dominant role.
- One way to think about [strategy] is in relation to [theory]. Another way is in relation to [alternative theory].
- The majority of the paper is devoted to formal empirical analyses.
- Our first empirical result is a striking confirmation that [technology] can [effect].
- Our second main empirical result is a contribution to the empirics of [mechanism].
- Overall, the regressions support the conclusion that [mechanism] is present.
- There is nothing to cause concern about our main results, although [limitation] does not let us provide strong additional support.
- The endogenous-[choice] aspect of the practice makes it different from previous [models].

## P1356：Intermediation and Competition in Search Markets

### Title / Abstract

标题把行业案例提升为一般机制：Intermediation and Competition in Search Markets，而不是 NYC Trade Waste Brokers。副标题 An Empirical Case Study 保留实证边界。

摘要第一句直接区分 direct effect 和 indirect effect。这个写法很值得学：一开头就告诉读者本文不是普通“中介省钱”文章，而是“中介改变竞争”的文章。

### Introduction

引言结构是 general problem -> mechanism -> rare data -> structural model -> counterfactual magnitudes。尤其是先解释中介如何筛走高搜索成本买家，再解释这会让直接市场更竞争。机制先行，数据随后。

### Literature Review

文献综述按模型部件组织：intermediation 文献给问题，search cost 文献给买方搜索，auction 文献给卖方成本识别。这种写法适合复杂方法论文，因为读者能看到每支文献在模型中负责哪一块。

### Model / Method Section

方法段先用白话说明识别难点：从一个价格分布无法区分高卖方成本和高买方搜索成本。然后再给 broker auction 与 direct search 两个市场共同识别的逻辑。顶刊结构模型常常需要先把 identification intuition 讲清楚，再进入公式。

### Results / Propositions

结果段不把参数表当终点，而把参数翻译成市场量级：搜索成本占总支出 8%-15%，移除 broker 让使用者多花 11.7%，非使用者也多花 2.5%，忽略外部性会漏掉 42%。

### Managerial Implications

政策含义写法聚焦“谁没有被私人收益覆盖”。中介创造的社会收益没有完全进入 broker 私人 payoff，因此可能 underprovided。这比泛泛说“中介有价值”更有理论味。

### Conclusion / Limitations

结论的 future research 紧扣舍弃假设：broker competition、service quality differentiation、dynamic buyer choice。每个方向都能对应模型当前边界。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Intermediaries can affect welfare both directly, by [mechanism], and indirectly, through [externality].
- Buyers who use [service] directly benefit from [better technology]. At the same time, [service] can also benefit other buyers through [market-level mechanism].
- This selection changes the composition of [market], increasing the proportion of [type].
- It is difficult to obtain data on [market] because of the very nature of these markets.
- The setting provides a rare opportunity to gain insight into [general phenomenon].
- The identification is complicated by the need to recover two primitive distributions from observed prices.
- Key to the identification strategy are two features of the setting.
- Without accounting for [externality], one would underestimate [welfare effect] by [magnitude].
- The results highlight the importance of [positive externality] created by [institution].

## P1571：Algorithmic Pricing and Competition

### Title / Abstract

标题把机制放在前面：Algorithmic Pricing and Competition，场景放在副标题。可学习动作：AI 论文标题不要只写“AI adoption”，而要写 AI 改变了哪个经济结果。

摘要按挑战推进：adoption unknown -> structural breaks；adoption endogenous -> headquarter adoption IV；competition mechanism -> non-monopoly and all-adopt oligopoly effects。

### Introduction

引言先讲政策担忧和理论无共识，再指出 no empirical evidence。接着列出三个 empirical challenges：adoption 不可观测、adoption 内生、margin 上升可能来自非竞争机制。后文每个方法都回应一个挑战。

这种结构很适合顶刊实证写作：不要急着展示结果，先告诉读者这个问题为什么难，难点越清楚，解决方案越有贡献感。

### Literature Review

文献综述承认 ambiguity：有些理论说算法可能合谋，有些说预测能力可能增加偏离激励。用理论无共识为实证必要性铺路。

### Method Section

方法段先解释四个 AP markers 与软件承诺的关系，再解释为什么“多个 marker 短窗口同时 break”比单个 break 更可信。变量构造不是技术细节，而是贡献的一部分。

IV 段把总部支持说成 adoption cost shifter：品牌提供 support/subsidies/training，降低站点采用成本。

### Results / Mechanism

结果链条层层排除替代解释：平均效应 -> 非垄断强、垄断弱 -> 全采用小寡头强、部分采用弱 -> 效应滞后 -> 更快跟随降价。这个“结果阶梯”值得写进 skill。

### Policy Discussion

政策讨论克制，明确说没有直接证据证明软件公司或品牌有反竞争行为。先限定证据性质，再讨论监管含义，避免过度指控。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The evolution of [technology] has raised concerns regarding [market outcome].
- At present, there is no theoretical consensus as to whether [mechanism] facilitates [outcome].
- As of yet, there is no empirical evidence linking [technology adoption] to [competition outcome].
- Investigating [effect] requires overcoming three important challenges.
- To overcome the first challenge, we [measurement strategy].
- Because [treatment] is endogenous, we instrument using [source of adoption cost variation].
- To isolate the effects on competition, we focus on the role of market structure.
- Markets where all [firms] adopt see [effect]; markets where only a subset adopts see no change.
- These findings suggest that [technology] affects competition rather than merely [alternative explanation].
- We find evidence consistent with algorithms learning that undercutting will not be profitable.

## P0250：The Dark Side of Voluntary Data Sharing

### Title / Abstract

标题用 “Dark Side” 把反直觉直接放到读者面前：voluntary data sharing 听起来像消费者保护，论文却要证明它可能伤害消费者。这个标题动作适合隐私、AI、平台治理论文：用一个常被认为正面的制度设计作为对象，再揭示它在均衡中的阴暗面。

摘要写法是 conventional wisdom -> model -> reversal -> policy implication。它先承认直觉，再说这种直觉需要被模型检验，最后给出反直觉监管含义。

### Introduction

引言把三方 tension 写清楚：消费者担心隐私，企业想用数据定价，监管者希望通过同意权保护消费者。随后引出本文问题：同意权本身是否真的保护消费者。这个写法比直接说“我们研究数据共享”更有顶刊味，因为问题来自制度设计内部的矛盾。

### Literature Review

文献综述最值得学的是“机制对比”而不是“主题归类”。作者与相近行为定价文献对比时强调：过去机制依赖消费者类型信号，本文消费者同质，机制来自购买数量、库存和未来价格之间的动态反馈。

### Model / Results

模型段先解释假设的现实来源：购买历史可以被企业看到，消费者可以撤回同意或匿名，隐私成本包含泄露风险和营销骚扰。结果段按隐私成本区间解释均衡，先给结论，再给 intuition paragraph。

### Policy Writing

政策写法不是泛泛说“监管要谨慎”，而是分别讨论禁止收集、自愿同意、降低隐私成本、分享奖励。每个政策都能映射到模型里的一个参数或决策变量。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Intuition suggests that [policy] only benefits [party].
- Whether or not this intuition holds demands scrutiny.
- We build a model in which [consumer] decides whether to [share information], while [firm] can use [information] to [price discriminate].
- Contrary to conventional wisdom, [consumer-friendly policy] can benefit [firm] at [consumer]'s expense.
- Though consumers are ex-post better off, they are ex-ante worse off because [firm strategic response].
- These findings caution policymakers that policies that seem purely beneficial can lead to unintended consequences.
- This result is in stark contrast to [closest literature].
- Our mechanism is completely different from theirs.

## P1878：Platform Disintermediation

### Title / Abstract

标题采用 object + mechanisms + remedies：Platform Disintermediation 是对象，Information Effects and Pricing Remedies 是机制和工具。摘要从佣金平台的商业模式进入 disintermediation，再列出佣金、信息质量、准入费、封禁等管理工具的反直觉效果。

### Introduction

引言用平台案例和 10-K 文件证明现实痛点，再把问题从“平台如何抓违规”转为“卖方为什么绕开”。这种转向让模型变量自然出现：佣金、支付保护、切换成本、买方风险信息。

### Literature Review

文献综述按模型部件组织：disintermediation 文献给问题，information design 文献给信号质量，platform pricing 文献给佣金与准入费。适合复杂建模论文学习。

### Results / Propositions

结果段善用成对概念解释复杂比较静态：back down vs double down，pricing power effect vs cost pass-on effect，trust effect vs disintermediation threat。这些短语把分段均衡翻译成读者能记住的机制。

### Managerial Implications

管理建议全部条件化：高信息环境不一定降佣金，低检测能力不一定封禁，低切换成本可考虑准入费。顶刊风格在于不给万能建议，而是明确适用条件。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Although [behavior] allows [party] to avoid [cost], it also leaves them exposed to [risk] and incurs [friction].
- Intuition might suggest that [platform] should respond by [standard action]. Yet we show that [opposite action] may be optimal when [condition].
- This result is a consequence of which transactions the platform chooses to capture value from.
- The platform can adopt one of two strategies: a back down strategy in which [action], or a double down strategy in which [action].
- An increase in [friction] generates two effects: a pricing power effect and a cost pass-on effect.
- The platform faces a clear trade-off: [benefit of information] versus [cost of information].
- Access fees reduce the incentive to disintermediate, but their revenue implications are less clear because [participation concern].
- Punitive measures can be counterproductive precisely in cases where the platform is most vulnerable to [behavior].

## P1653：Vertical Competition on a Common Platform

### Title / Abstract

标题把现实平台问题抽象成理论对象：Vertical Competition on a Common Platform。摘要第一句定义 common infrastructure as common attributes，然后按机制列出 divergence、top-down、nonmonotonic platform response 和 win-win-win。

### Introduction

引言用 Steam 和 iOS 进入，先解释平台基础设施为什么是消费者感知质量的一部分，再引出高低质量厂商如何差异化。现实例子服务于变量 E 和 q_i，而不是装饰。

### Literature Review

文献综述用两支文献对接模型：common attributes 文献对应 E，platform infrastructure 文献对应平台投资和佣金。写法适合学习如何把两个成熟文献拼成一个新机制。

### Results / Propositions

结果段用 bottom-up impact 和 top-down impact 两个短语制造记忆点。复杂的一阶条件最后被翻译成“共同属性先增强低端，佣金先压制高端”。

### Managerial Implications

管理含义落在联合决策：平台不能只调基础设施，也不能只调佣金；GenAI 降低开发成本后，基础设施投入可能非单调。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We consider a platform whose [infrastructure] constitutes common attributes for [vertically differentiated products].
- Vendors face unique opportunities and challenges on a common platform.
- Conventional wisdom suggests that [firms] will [standard response]. Surprisingly, we find that [asymmetric response].
- This happens because [platform decision] exerts a bottom-up influence, with a stronger impact on [low-end player] than on [high-end player].
- A higher [commission] discourages both vendors from offering [quality] in a top-down pattern.
- The platform responds to [technology improvement] by jointly adjusting [infrastructure] and [commission].
- When [cost] declines but remains high, [platform action]; when [cost] is already low, [opposite platform action].
- These strategic responses lead to a win-win-win outcome, while also strengthening [platform]'s market position.

## P1843：Platform Disintermediation with Repeated Transactions

### Title / Abstract

标题把已有议题 disintermediation 和新情境 repeated transactions 组合。摘要按 problem -> pricing response -> screening result -> buyer welfare caveat 推进，主线很紧。

### Introduction

引言用 Airbnb、CoachUp、Preply 等重复交易例子引入，再把平台便利收益具体化为保险、支付托管、排课、AI 项目管理工具。现实例子直接对应模型变量 b。

### Literature Review

文献综述抓最接近文献 Hagiu and Wright 的 one-off transaction 对比，说明本文因为 repeated transactions 推出相反结论：绕开有时利好平台。

### Results / Propositions

结果按异质性递进：无异质性 -> 购买频率异质性 -> 便利收益异质性 -> 两者同时存在。每一步只加一个机制，所以读者能看清结论为何反转。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示具体落到收费和治理：front-loaded pricing、投资便利收益、防 early leakage、referral fee 加 transaction fee，而不是只说“防止绕开”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Buyers and sellers often go on to transact with each other multiple times.
- The platform reacts to [threat] by using a front-loaded pricing scheme.
- Disintermediation is both a threat and an opportunity.
- The platform can use [outside option] to screen users' private information about [valuation].
- Buyers are not necessarily better off when they can [exercise choice] because [platform] adjusts its fees.
- Heterogeneity in [dimension A] harms the platform, whereas heterogeneity in [dimension B] can benefit it.
- The ability to disintermediate raises the buyer's willingness to pay for the first transaction and lowers it for later transactions.
- Managers should focus on preventing early disintermediation rather than eliminating all later off-platform relationships.

## P1903：Automated Targeted Bidding for Sponsored Ads

### Title / Abstract

标题直接包含方法对象、广告场景和平台场景：automated targeted bidding、sponsored ads、e-commerce platforms。摘要用连续反直觉句式推进：动态出价可能提价、不一定提高转化率、平台价值可能来自销售收入而非广告收入。

### Introduction

引言先用 Amazon 广告收入和卖家管理 PPC 困难说明现实重要性，再用行业引用解释平台 AI 信息优势。随后提出关键 gap：电商广告估值由卖家价格内生决定，不同于 standalone auction。

### Literature Review

文献对话不是只说“我们研究 AI 广告”，而是把 auction information、sponsored search 和 platform marketplace 连接到一个核心机制：seller pricing and sponsored ad decisions interact endogenously。

### Results / Propositions

结果段不断反转 conventional wisdom：更精准不一定更高转化，广告工具不一定提高广告收入，按转化付费不一定是好补救。每个反转都回到价格和广告估值的内生互动。

### Managerial Implications

管理含义聚焦收入结构：平台要看自己靠广告还是靠销售抽佣赚钱；卖家要理解动态出价改变最优价格；监管者要关注算法是否软化竞争。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Sponsored ad auctions on e-commerce platforms do not occur in a vacuum.
- What is new in our context is that [valuations] are determined endogenously through [strategic decisions].
- Contrary to conventional wisdom, [AI tool] need not lead to [expected performance outcome].
- The platform's main source of value may come not from [direct revenue channel] but from [indirect revenue channel].
- This result is due to the endogenous interaction between [decision A] and [decision B].
- Sellers may strategically raise prices to limit their competition for [targeted consumers].
- Higher information precision hurts [revenue source A] but boosts [revenue source B].
- A remedy may be worse than the problem it is intended to solve.

## P1668：The Power of Disagreement

### Title / Abstract

标题采用“概念化短语 + 方法 + 场景”的结构：The Power of Disagreement 提供记忆点，A Field Experiment 提供可信度，Loan Evaluations 限定边界。适合学习如何把一个看似负面的行为写成理论机制。

摘要按 problem -> tension -> mechanism -> method -> findings -> contribution 推进。关键写作动作是把人机协作的价值从 adoption 转向 disagreement timing：不是人是否听算法，而是人是否在算法错的时候不听。

### Introduction

引言最强的 tension 是：如果人只是跟随算法，为什么还需要人在回路中？这个问题把“算法很准”这个常识推到自我矛盾处，从而自然引出 human-in-the-loop 的真正价值。

可迁移写法：先承认技术优势，再指出单纯依赖技术无法解释人的剩余价值，最后提出人和技术互动中的新机制。

### Literature Review

文献综述按机制重排：collaboration performance 说明现象，algorithm aversion 说明既有解释，decision-rule heterogeneity 和 disagreement 提供本文变量。它不是按“AI 文献很多”铺陈，而是每支文献都服务一个变量或路径。

### Model / Method Section

方法写法先解释业务场景，再解释实验因素为什么来自理论，再介绍变量和回归。变量命名本身有理论感：organic divergence、collaborative disagreement、decision augmentation。顶刊风味来自把业务日志变量命名成可迁移概念。

### Results / Propositions

结果段不是报显著性，而是持续区分“分歧的数量”和“分歧的质量”。作者用 cautious but confident 的语气解释反直觉结果：Consistency 触发分歧却削弱价值，Contradiction 触发分歧并增强价值。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示落到 disagreement quality：企业不应只提高算法采纳率，而应设计解释界面、员工培训和复核流程，让人基于算法质量线索而不是过度自信来 override。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Although [technology] can outperform humans in [task], simply asking humans to follow [technology] does not explain why humans should remain in the loop.
- The key question is not whether humans disagree with algorithms, but when such disagreement creates value.
- Disagreement can be either a source of augmentation or a symptom of miscalibrated confidence.
- We shift attention from [adoption/trust] to the formation and effectiveness of [behavior].
- A higher level of [behavior] does not necessarily imply a higher level of [value].
- The value of explainability lies not only in fostering trust but also in enabling informed disagreement.
- Managers should monitor the quality, not merely the frequency, of [human override / intervention].
- [Signal] helps decision makers evaluate the appropriateness of [algorithm/tool/policy] for a specific case.

## P0064：Decision Bias in the Newsvendor Problem

### Title / Abstract

标题把边界写得很干净：Decision Bias 是机制，Newsvendor Problem 是经典对象，Known Demand Distribution 排除预测问题，Experimental Evidence 标明方法。适合学习如何用标题控制读者预期。

摘要的动作是：经典模型定义 -> 标准解已知 -> 行为未知 -> 两个实验 -> 系统偏差 -> 排除替代解释 -> 提出机制。它几乎是一篇实验论文的最小完整骨架。

### Introduction

引言先把 newsvendor 的现实场景铺开，再指出理论和现实之间的断裂：expected profit-maximizing order quantity is well known, but little is known about how managers actually make these decisions。这个 gap 不是“没人研究某背景”，而是“规范知识不知道能否落地”。

### Theory / Literature Section

理论部分按 competing explanations 写，而不是按文献年代写。每个解释都对应一个可检验方向：risk aversion、waste aversion、stockout aversion、ex-post inventory error、anchoring。适合学习实验论文如何让文献服务识别。

### Results

结果顺序很讲究：先看 first inventory decisions，避免反馈和经验干扰；再看 average decisions，说明稳定偏差；再看 adjustment patterns，检验学习和追逐需求。这个顺序可以迁移到所有多轮实验。

### Managerial Implications

管理建议不是泛泛说“加强培训”，而是提出流程改造：separate forecasting from inventory decision、automate conversion、change incentives、provide alternative profit feedback。同时承认经理可能 game the system，这种克制让建议更可信。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The expected profit-maximizing solution is well known, but little is known about how managers actually make these decisions.
- Choices systematically deviate from those that maximize expected profit.
- This pattern is not consistent with [explanation A], [explanation B], or [explanation C].
- Two explanations are consistent with the data.
- Feedback and training did not mitigate [decision errors].
- A better understanding of actual behavior may reveal that traditional assumptions need modification.
- The goal is to improve decision quality by accounting for systematic bias, rather than necessarily correcting the bias itself.
- Experimental methodology should be used to study other critical models in operations management.

## P1651：Privacy and Polarization

### Title / Abstract

标题把两个政策讨论中的大词放在一起：Privacy and Polarization，再用 An Inference-Based Framework 限定机制。它不是情绪化讨论隐私好坏，而是承诺用 inference 解释两者关系。

摘要按现实冲突 -> 信息环境比较 -> 反直觉结果 -> 竞争机制 -> 贡献推进。核心写法是把隐私监管的影响写成 firms' equilibrium responses，而不是只写 direct effect。

### Introduction

引言先用 GDPR、CCPA、Apple ATT 等现实政策建立重要性，再引用已有实证结果：general interest publishers 在隐私监管下收入损失更大。这个事实自然引出 niche content 更容易推断用户类型，从而进入模型机制。

### Literature Review

文献综述用三支文献搭桥：media markets / behavioral targeting / privacy regulation。每段都不是泛泛回顾，而是指出本文如何把 privacy 写成 inference problem，并内生化媒体定位。

### Model Section

模型写法很适合学习：先解释 0、1/2、1 的现实含义，再给效用；先定义 privacy 为条件方差，再说明它如何进入利润。每个数学对象都先有现实解释。

### Results / Propositions

命题递进清楚：Lemma 1 解决广告后验均值，Lemma 3 说明 niche choice 更 privacy-reducing，Proposition 1 给 behavioral targeting 基准，Proposition 2 给 contextual targeting 反转，Proposition 5 给竞争放大。

### Policy / Managerial Implications

政策写法克制：不是说隐私监管错了，而是指出 unintended consequences through equilibrium responses。顶刊风格在于指出 second-order effect，而不是直接站队。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We characterize privacy as an inference problem.
- The firm faces a tradeoff between the quantity and quality of impressions.
- A more niche positioning reduces demand but improves the firm's ability to infer consumer types.
- Privacy regulation can have unintended consequences through firms' equilibrium responses.
- Competition strengthens the inference motive through an inferential complementarity effect.
- This mechanism is distinct from the commonly discussed link between personalization and polarization.
- Content positioning becomes a strategic tool for consumer type inference.

## P1030：Platform Green vs Non-Green Competition

### Title / Abstract

标题信息量很大，明确包含 platform role、green vs non-green competition、blockchain transparency、selling contract selection。它适合 IJPE/POM 类应用建模论文，但不如顶刊标题克制。

摘要覆盖完整但 findings 偏多。可学习的是多结果论文如何按 greenness、supplier profits、platform profits、supply chain efficiency 排列；需要警惕的是主线容易被结果清单冲淡。

### Introduction

引言从平台 Scope 3 emissions 和 Amazon/Zalando/Best Buy 案例进入，现实动机清楚。案例直接对应模型中的 reselling、agency 和绿色实践，这一点可学习。

### Literature Review

文献综述用 Table 1 定位本文：selling formats、supply chain members、greenness、competition、blockchain technology。对组合型建模论文，用表格显示“我把哪些成熟元素合在一起”很有效。

### Model Section

模型部分用四种模式 RR/AA/RA/AR 展开，每个模式只改变定价权、佣金和成本承担。复杂多模式论文必须靠 notation table 和模式表降低读者负担。

### Results / Propositions

结果最好按对象而非命题顺序阅读：greenness、green supplier、non-green supplier、platform、whole supply chain。写自己论文时要从大量比较静态中筛出主机制，避免变成“所有参数都比较了一遍”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Blockchain transparency improves consumers' perceived greenness but also reshapes upstream competition.
- The optimal selling contract depends on who controls pricing power and who bears green investment costs.
- A technology that benefits product verification may create free-riding opportunities for non-green suppliers.
- Each contract form can become optimal under specific market conditions.
- The platform should tailor selling formats to supplier types rather than adopt a uniform contract.

## P1678：Data and Algorithms in Marketplace Analytics

### Title / Abstract

标题用 “Data and Algorithms” 把平台技术能力拆成两个资产，再用 “Strategic Disclosure of Competitiveness” 点出机制。可学习之处是：不要把题名写成泛泛的 AI analytics，而要直接暴露理论张力。

摘要动作是 data boom -> open/restrictive policy puzzle -> platform has control despite third-party entry -> model two intertwined decisions -> over-optimistic algorithms -> adoption failure -> open data -> policy implication。它把第三方进入写成 puzzle，而不是背景事实。

### Introduction

引言先给 Amazon、eBay、Alibaba 和 Jungle Scout、Helium 10 等真实工具，再提出 friend-or-foe question。随后先承认读者直觉：平台有 first-party data 和自有算法，似乎应封锁第三方；再说明本文机制：算法目标不一致导致卖家不信任。

### Literature Review

文献综述的关键动作是“归类后立刻差异化”：本文属于平台信息共享、平台治理和 Bayesian persuasion，但差异分别是 analytics adoption、algorithmic incentive 和 signal structure 采用前不可观察。

### Model Section

模型写法适合模仿：先给卖家价格竞争和未知竞争强度，再定义平台和第三方 analytics 的差异。平台是 superior data + manipulable algorithm；第三方是 limited data + seller-aligned algorithm。两个维度足以支撑全文。

### Results / Propositions

命题写法围绕读者疑问推进。Proposition 1 先证明平台算法偏乐观，Corollary 1 说明卖家想要更准算法，Proposition 2 再回答为什么较弱第三方能帮助平台。反直觉结果后立即解释 adoption incentives，是顶刊理论文常见节奏。

### Managerial / Policy Implications

管理启示分成平台、卖家、第三方和监管者。好的地方在于每类主体都有具体决策：开放数据、选择工具、证明独立性、限制 API。政策部分用 unintended consequences through equilibrium responses，而不是简单站队。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The key question is why a platform may accommodate third-party providers despite weakening its own data advantage.
- We distinguish between the quality of data and the incentives embedded in algorithm design.
- Superior data access does not necessarily translate into greater adoption when users question the provider's incentives.
- The platform's first-best outcome may be infeasible because sellers rationally anticipate algorithmic manipulation.
- Third-party analytics can serve as a commitment substitute when platform-provided analytics lacks credibility.
- The effect of technological advancement is nonmonotonic: it first enables adoption and later intensifies substitution.
- Restrictions intended to protect consumers may have unintended consequences through platforms' equilibrium responses.
- The relevant metric is not only average accuracy, but the allocation of accuracy across states.

## P0266：The Illusion of Authenticity in Online Reviews

### Title / Abstract

标题先用 “The Illusion of Authenticity” 给现象命名，再用副标题交代理论机制 truth bias 和场景变量 valence。实验论文如果能给核心偏差一个清楚名字，读者更容易记住。

摘要动作是 fake review prevalence / automated detection -> consumer perception gap -> truth-default theory + deception motives -> five experiments -> platform priorities。它没有堆实验细节，而是强调理论逻辑和证据范围。

### Introduction

引言的关键转折是 “In contrast to a technical perspective...” 这类写法。作者没有否定算法检测文献，而是说本文从 consumer perception perspective 补充一个缺口。这种写法适合在已有大文献中开新角度。

### Theory / Hypotheses

假设链条很顺：truth-default theory 推出 truth bias，truth bias 推出 veracity effect，deception motives 推出 valence effect，cognitive accessibility 推出 automaticity。每个假设都不是孤立猜测。

### Study Sequence

五个实验不是重复，而是逐步回应反驳：Study 1 建立效应，Study 2 排除程序混淆，Study 3 排除文本内容混淆，Study 4 验证 deception motives，Study 5 验证自动性。写实验论文时，每个 study 都必须有明确任务。

### Results / Practical Implications

结果写法强调“整体准确率接近随机，但偏差结构稳定”。实践启示具体到平台：用户举报不足、删除假评未必提升信任、可用 warning labels 和 fact-checker badges、优先关注假负评。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Despite growing research on [technical detection], little is known about how consumers judge [object] when its veracity is unknown.
- We approach the issue from a consumer perception perspective.
- Even when participants knew that [base rate], they classified too many messages as [category].
- The effect is not driven by [confound], because it disappears when [diagnostic cue] is no longer useful.
- The results provide converging evidence across five experiments.
- Increased awareness of [risk] may not translate into skepticism toward individual instances.
- User reporting is unlikely to be sufficient and should be supplemented with platform-side interventions.

## P1551：Luxury Quality Disclosure

### Title / Abstract

中文标题用问句 “保持神秘还是公开透明？” 直接呈现管理困惑。若按英文顶刊风格，可压成 “Mystique or Transparency: Quality Disclosure for Status Goods”。最值得突出的 finding 是 luxury disclosure violates unraveling。

### Introduction

引言从 Aura、Tiffany、Honest By 等透明实践和其他品牌保留神秘感的差异进入。这个现实对比很好，但主线可以更早聚焦到 “quality disclosure has opposite effects on functional utility and status utility”。

### Literature Review

文献综述两支线：奢侈品地位效用和质量披露。可学习的是用成熟效用函数接入新决策变量；需要警惕的是中文论文容易把文献综述写成罗列，顶刊写法应更突出最近文献差异。

### Model / Results

模型主线是 threshold disclosure。结果最好按三层写：高质量披露/低质量隐藏、分散渠道披露不足、奢侈品不同于普通产品。最强结果是即使披露无成本，奢侈品也不完全披露。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Transparency can enhance functional utility while diluting the mystique that sustains status utility.
- Unlike ordinary products, status goods may benefit from withholding information even when disclosure is costless.
- The disclosure threshold captures the brand's trade-off between perceived excellence and exclusivity.
- Decentralized channels weaken disclosure incentives because retailers appropriate part of the disclosure benefit.
- Quality disclosure for luxury brands is not an all-or-nothing transparency problem but a selective signaling problem.

## P2031：Backfiring AI in Workplace

### Title / Abstract

标题用 “Backfiring AI?” 直接形成反直觉。摘要先承认 AI 提效和知识转移预期，再指出竞争工作场所中 AI 可能削弱高绩效员工激励，最后给出政策含义。它不是泛泛说 AI 有风险，而是把风险定位为 employee incentive issues。

### Introduction

引言先铺 AI 作为 general-purpose technology 的乐观背景，再转入 PFP 薪酬和员工异质性。好的地方是：AI 的优点“学习和扩散 best practices”同时也是问题来源。

### Model Section

模型变量 tangible/intangible skills 都先用销售、软件开发、仓储等例子解释，再给数学形式。AI 论文抽象变量必须先有现实可感的动作，否则读者会觉得只是参数游戏。

### Results / Policy

结果按个人产出、企业利润、员工福利、补救政策推进。最值得学的是把管理者直觉方案 guaranteed salary 放进模型，证明它可能失败。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- AI-facilitated knowledge transfer can alter the competitive dynamics among employees.
- The very skills that once created a relative advantage may become codified and diffused through AI.
- The payoff from AI deployment depends not only on technological efficacy but also on workforce composition.
- Pursuing the most advanced AI system is not always optimal.
- Simple compensation guarantees may fail because they dampen competitive incentives.
- Firms should calibrate AI capabilities to balance skill augmentation with employee motivation.

## P1664：Strategic Value of Data Sharing

### Title / Abstract

标题把 data sharing 写成 strategic value，而不是 efficiency value。摘要最有记忆点的表达是 “competitor into a coopetitor” 和 “creates codependence”。

### Introduction

引言从 big-tech 追逐跨市场数据进入，提出 specialist 应 hoard 还是 share 的问题。现实案例和理论 puzzle 结合紧。

### Model / Results

理论写法很强：先用 Lemma 1 证明固定投资下 sharing 有害，承认直觉；再用 Proposition 1 在投资响应下反转。这个“先承认直觉，再反转直觉”的结构值得学习。

### Policy Implications

政策结论克制：数据共享可能帮助小企业面对大科技，也可能软化竞争伤害消费者。不是单边站队。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Counter to this intuition, a specialist may willingly share data with an intruding generalist.
- Data sharing strategically creates codependence between firms.
- The generalist becomes a stakeholder in the specialist's continued success.
- Data can substitute for direct investment in product quality.
- Data sharing softens competition and transforms a competitor into a coopetitor.
- Although firms benefit from data sharing, consumers may be worse off.

## P1032：Prepopulated Search Bars

### Title / Abstract

标题用问句直接提出管理问题，并用 “A Field Experiment” 给识别设计背书。它适合学习“具体对象 + 具体行为结果 + 方法标签”的标题写法。

摘要按 problem -> gap -> method -> differentiated findings -> contribution 推进。最值得学的是每个 finding 都有数字和对照对象：10.4% purchase incidence、8.8% spending、21% purchase incidence、17% spending，而不是只说 significantly improves。

### Introduction

引言先用电商市场规模、搜索框使用比例、搜索框贡献收入和页面浏览这些现实数字建立重要性。然后用 Amazon、Walmart、Temu、Taobao 的界面差异说明企业已经在做不同选择。这个动作能把小 UI 问题写成真实管理问题。

### Literature Review

作者先承认 consumer search 文献已经覆盖 advertising、ranking、search environment、keywords、refinement tools，再指出 prepopulated search bar 这个早期入口尚未被研究。接着把它和 product recommendation 区分开：传统推荐通常发生在旅程中段，预填搜索框发生在 query 之前。

### Method / Identification

方法段写得像审稿人问答：random assignment、same advertisements and menus、same ranking algorithm、search bar as sole querying portal、unlikely user awareness。每一句都对应一个识别威胁。

### Results

结果写法的亮点是用 seemingly contradictory finding 制造解释需求：personalized keywords reduce queries but increase purchases。随后通过 direct effect 和 spillover effect 拆分，把矛盾变成 insight。

### Managerial Implications

管理启示分三类角色：customers、platform managers、policymakers。平台经理的建议落到 regularly updating trending keywords、balancing personalization and serendipity；监管建议落到 transparency、data use、excessive personalization。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Search tools, once deemed supplementary, have become central to the consumer journey from initial search to final purchase.
- Despite the extensiveness of this literature stream, little is known about how [interface feature] affects [early-stage behavior].
- This intervention creates an exogenous shock that alters [default configuration].
- The effect is driven primarily by [direct channel] rather than changes in [alternative channel].
- The findings reveal both a heightened-focus effect and a crowding-out effect.
- A design that increases conversion may nevertheless narrow consumer exploration.
- Managers should balance relevance with opportunities for diverse discovery and serendipity.

## P1616：Tax-Induced Inequalities

### Title / Abstract

标题不是 “The Effect of Airbnb Taxes”，而是 “Tax-Induced Inequalities”。它直接命名机制和贡献：税收诱发的不平等。摘要同时覆盖方法、核心异质性、机制和处方，信息密度高但主线明确。

### Introduction

引言先承认监管合理性：Airbnb 确实影响酒店和住房市场，收税并非没有道理。然后转入 target/nontarget listings 的异质性，指出统一税率可能和政策目标错位。这个写法避免把文章写成单纯反监管，而是写成“好政策也可能有 unintended consequences”。

### Literature Review

三支文献都服务主线：Airbnb 文献说明平台和住房市场背景，tax literature 引出 tax aversion，causal ML 文献说明为什么要研究 heterogeneous treatment effects。文献综述没有变成方法炫耀。

### Method Section

方法从传统 DID 写起，再解释为什么需要 causal forest。读者先理解基准识别，再接受复杂方法。适合学习如何在顶刊中引入机器学习：必须先说明它解决什么识别或处方问题。

### Results / Mechanism

机制段采用排除式结构：not differential price adjustment、not listing dropout、not price sensitivity、but discriminatory tax aversion。这个结构非常适合实证论文回应审稿人替代解释。

### Managerial / Policy Implications

管理启示给出数字：optimal price response is -7.2%；discriminatory tax rate for nontarget listings is 6%。这比“平台和监管者应优化政策”更像 OM 顶刊写法。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A regulation intended to level the playing field may generate unintended distributional consequences.
- The effects are disproportionately more pronounced for [nontarget group] than for [target group].
- We rule out supply-side explanations before turning to demand-side mechanisms.
- The mechanism is not tax aversion per se, but discriminatory tax aversion across market participants.
- We leverage the estimated treatment effects to develop data-driven prescriptions.
- A uniform policy may fail when market participants and consumer perceptions are heterogeneous.
- Prescriptive analytics can translate causal estimates into actionable pricing and policy recommendations.

## P1898：Cold Start to Improve Market Thickness

### Title / Abstract

标题把研究目标、平台场景和方法组合一次性交代清楚：Cold Start -> Market Thickness -> Online Advertising Platforms -> Data-Driven Algorithms and Field Experiments。摘要的中心句是 trade-off between short-term revenue and long-term market thickness。

### Introduction

引言先用广告行业规模建立重要性，再指出传统 cold start 文献只看 CTR/CVR learning。作者用 Figure 1 展示早期转化和后续留存关系，把算法问题转成 market thickness 问题。

### Literature Review

文献综述三线并行：cold start、bandit algorithms、field experiments。每条线都说明本文不仅补理论空白，也解决实践不可实施或实验偏误问题。

### Model / Algorithm Section

模型段先讲 DSP 业务流程，再引入公式。算法段反复强调 minimal adjustments：只需给新广告加 shadow bid。复杂数学因此服务落地，而不是脱离平台现实。

### Field Experiment

实验段的写法很值得学：先说明普通 ad-side 和 UV-side randomization 为什么会错，再提出 two-sided design。这样新实验设计不是炫技，而是识别问题的必然结果。

### Results

结果分 short-term、long-term、global simulation 三层推进。短期报收益和成本，长期报留存、CTR 和收入，仿真报全平台收益。这个结构适合算法干预类论文。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Traditional approaches focus on [statistical objective], but overlook the economic role of [market-level outcome].
- The fundamental trade-off is between short-term revenue and long-term market thickness.
- We develop an end-to-end approach that is theoretically sound and practically feasible.
- The algorithm can be implemented with minimal adjustments to the existing system.
- One-sided experiments violate SUTVA in two-sided platforms and can generate substantial bias.
- The short-term loss is more than compensated by long-term gains in market thickness.

## P0671：Promoting Mobile Payment with Price Incentives

### Title / Abstract

标题朴素但准：对象是 mobile payment，工具是 price incentives，动作是 promoting。摘要先报 proprietary data 和 10 million transactions，再报 cash dominant but mobile growing，最后展开促销效果、持续性、成本收益、异质性和来源分解。

### Introduction

引言采用“大背景 -> 小场景”的降落：全球支付方式从现金转向非现金，移动支付对消费者、商户、银行和社会都有价值；然后落到中国杂货店 POS 场景，解释为什么这里特别适合观察 cash-to-mobile substitution。

### Literature Review

文献综述按差异组织，而不是按作者堆砌：已有文献多研究 cash/card，本文研究 mobile/cash；已有激励常由商户或卡发行方提供，本文激励由移动支付平台提供；已有研究较少同时看激励形式、习惯和价格敏感性。

### Method Section

方法段先证明 setting-to-question fit：低金额杂货交易、现金占优、银行卡极少，所以适合研究移动支付如何替代现金。然后再解释 LPM、固定效应和变量构建。这种顺序让方法不是孤立公式，而是从场景自然长出来。

### Results

结果段的排列很值得复用：是否有效 -> 是否持续 -> 是否回本 -> 对谁更强 -> 来源是什么。每一步都回应一个管理者或审稿人会追问的问题。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A shift from [old technology] to [new technology] can generate benefits for consumers, firms, intermediaries, and society, but adoption remains uneven in [setting].
- Our empirical context is particularly appropriate because [baseline behavior is dominant] and [the intervention varies over time].
- We exploit [platform-provided incentives] to examine substitution between [old choice] and [new choice].
- The effect persists for a few days after the incentive expires, suggesting short-run habit formation rather than a purely contemporaneous response.
- The incentives are unlikely to be financed by the additional transactions alone, indicating that providers may internalize broader ecosystem benefits.
- The steering effect is stronger when consumers have already built up habits and when they are more price sensitive.
- Additional usage comes from both substitution from the incumbent option and transactions that would not otherwise have occurred.

## P1934：Quality Verification in the Presence of Review Bias

### Title / Abstract

标题把两个核心对象直接并列：quality verification 和 review bias。摘要的推进是旧工具 -> 新信息环境 -> 研究问题 -> 两种时机 -> 条件化结果。它没有把评论写成纯坏东西，而是写成 accessible but biased，这种平衡语气更像顶刊。

### Introduction

引言的 tension 很清楚：读者可能以为 online reviews 会让 traditional verification obsolete；作者立刻转向 different perspective，指出评论偏差使认证和评论互补。案例也服务模型：Agoda/Airbnb 对应信息共存，食品安全回应对应 responsive verification。

### Literature Review

文献综述按三条线组织：consumer reviews、voluntary disclosure、quality verification。每条线都说明本文不是简单替代，而是加入 review bias 和 timing，形成新的策略问题。

### Model Section

模型段先解释两类信息渠道的现实差异，再写两期时序和数学设定。公式不多，但每个公式都有经济功能：效用给需求，$R=q+\epsilon$ 给评论偏差，$q\ge b$ 给认证信号。

### Results

命题后的直觉段写得值得学：不是只解释阈值，而是把阈值翻译成情境。例如 responsive verification 不是差评越差越做，而是评论负面但仍可被解释为偏差时才做。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- One might expect [new information channel] to render [traditional signal] obsolete. We offer a different perspective.
- [Signal A], while valuable and accessible, can be biased and unreliable because of [source of heterogeneity].
- [Signal B] is costly but credible, and therefore can serve as a counterbalance to [biased signal].
- The timing of [information action] matters because it determines whether the firm shapes expectations ex ante or corrects beliefs ex post.
- The firm preserves the upside potential of favorable reviews while mitigating the downside of negatively biased reviews.
- The value of verification is highest when reviews are negative enough to distort beliefs but not so negative that verification is infeasible.
- The two formats serve different functions: one improves overall inference, whereas the other selectively corrects adverse inferences.

## P2025：Dynamic Pricing and Product Quality with Review-Driven Learning

### Title / Abstract

标题把 dynamic pricing、product quality、review-driven learning 三个核心机制并列。摘要采用 gap -> model -> mechanism -> non-intuitive result -> welfare -> robustness 的推进，特别强调 review volume 和 valence 共同形成 learning precision effect。

### Introduction

引言先承认评论对消费者购买的重要性，再指出 supply-side implications remain less understood。这个 gap 很干净：不是重复“评论影响消费者”，而是转向“企业如何从评论中学习并改产品”。

### Literature Review

文献综述的关键词是 joint endogenization：pricing、quality、review generation、quality adjustment。作者持续说明本文不是在旧模型里加一个评论变量，而是让评论生成和企业产品迭代互相反馈。

### Model Section

模型段最值得学的是拆分 product-based quality 和 market-based quality。前者企业可控，后者只能从评论中学到。这个概念拆分让后面所有公式都有现实含义。

### Results

结果按三层 benchmark 展开：无评论、有评论但无质量调整、有评论且可质量调整。每一层新增一个现实机制，并说明均衡如何改变，适合仿写理论模型主分析结构。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- While prior work has emphasized the demand-side role of [information], its supply-side implications remain less understood.
- We distinguish between [firm-controlled attribute] and [market-based perception], the latter of which can only be learned through consumption.
- Review volume and valence jointly determine the precision with which market-based quality can be inferred.
- The firm may sacrifice first-period profit to generate more informative reviews and improve second-period decisions.
- The option to adjust quality amplifies the informational value of reviews.
- Contrary to conventional intuition, the firm may raise rather than lower the introductory price when [condition].
- Review-driven learning can create a win-win outcome for both firms and consumers.

## P0031：Managing Outpatient Service with Strategic Walk-ins

### Title / Abstract

标题直接点出服务场景和战略行为：outpatient service + strategic walk-ins。摘要按现实管理难题 -> 三个 operational levers -> stylized queueing model -> 条件化发现推进。

### Introduction

引言用 high fever 和 runny nose 的对比解释急症与轻症患者差异，再把 appointment delay 和 in-clinic waiting 两种等待尺度讲清楚。现实画面先行，公式后置。

### Literature Review

文献综述把 healthcare OM、service OM、strategic queueing 分开，并在每支文献中说明本文新增双渠道、两阶段选择、容量分配和信息披露设计。

### Model Section

模型段最值得学的是用 disengagement cost 抽象系统设计。real-time 和 asynchronous 不再只是技术名称，而是患者看到 delay 后是否容易反悔的行为摩擦。

### Results

结果用 regime 分类降低复杂度：SnB、SnW、SWB 分别对应患者行为集合。复杂队列均衡被翻译成“谁预约、谁 walk in、谁 balk”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Patients face a trade-off between waiting in two different time scales: [delay type A] and [delay type B].
- We capture the difference between [system A] and [system B] through a single behavioral friction, [parameter].
- Neither design is universally more efficient; the preferred design depends on [environmental factor 1] and [behavioral factor 2].
- The system can protect one channel from congestion, but doing so may increase lost demand.
- There is no one-size-fits-all model; the best operational lever depends on the practice environment.

## P0007：Before It's Too Late: Product Recall Delays and Policy Design

### Title / Abstract

标题采用“紧迫感短语 + 精确研究对象”的两段式结构。Abstract 用 Problem definition、Practical relevance、Methodology、Results、Managerial implications 分块，适合学习 MSOM 风格的结构化摘要。

### Introduction

引言先用产品复杂度和安全标准提高铺垫现实重要性，再用 GM 和 Toyota 案例说明企业可能拖延甚至操纵调查。之后用三个问题收束：是否努力调查、是否延迟召回、政府如何抑制长延迟。

### Literature Review

文献综述嵌在贡献段中，先给已有召回文献贴功能标签：firm value、demand、marketing activities、ex ante operations strategies，再说明本文转向 ex post investigation and recall timing decisions。

### Model Section

模型段先解释 mass media 和 word of mouth，再写 Bass hazard function。最值得学的是 timing 图：$t_C$、$t_I$、$t_R$ 三个时间点让复杂危机管理问题变成可解的动态优化。

### Results

命题结果用 Case A/B/C1/C2 决策树表达。复杂阈值条件被翻译成生命周期阶段、内外部影响强弱、利润成本比三类现实条件。

### Managerial Implications

政策启示按工具组织：information disclosure、penalty design、investigation effort deployment、investigation assistance。每个工具都对应一个模型机制，避免泛泛说“加强监管”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- When [risk event] occurs, firms must make two linked decisions: whether to [investigate/verify] and when to [disclose/recall/respond].
- Although stakeholders expect timely action, anecdotal evidence suggests that firms may remain passive or strategically delay response.
- We model [decision] over the product life cycle by incorporating [event] into a diffusion framework.
- The firm trades off the immediate demand loss from public disclosure against the growing cost of continued delay.
- A policy that penalizes observed delay may shorten delay duration, but it need not improve investigation effort when firms can manipulate the investigation process.
- Regulators should monitor not only the timing of public action but also the process through which firms identify the underlying risk.

## P0134：Product Sourcing and Distribution Strategies under Supply Disruption and Recall Risks

### Title / Abstract

标题把两个决策 sourcing/distribution 和两个风险 disruption/recall 同时压缩进去。摘要按现实风险 -> 两种分销策略 -> 策略分区 -> 相关性发现推进。

### Introduction

引言最值得学的是先承认 common intuition：multiple sourcing mitigates supply uncertainty，再指出该直觉对 recall risk 未必成立，因为 recall occurs after products reach customers。

### Literature Review

文献综述按 product recall、quality analytical models、supply uncertainty 三支展开，每支最后回到本文差异：用 distribution strategy 处理 recall risk，并区分 recall 与 disruption。

### Model Section

模型段采用总利润分解：$T=\pi-R-L$。这种写法让复杂运营模型变得可读，每一节都只负责解释一个成本或收益来源。

### Results

结果从基础 trade-off 到阈值策略地图，再到相关性反转。Proposition 2 负责建立直觉，Theorem 1 负责给管理分类，Proposition 5-6 负责制造理论惊喜。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Although [strategy] is commonly used to mitigate [risk A], it may not be equally effective for [risk B].
- The key distinction is that [risk A] is resolved before sales, whereas [risk B] materializes after products reach customers.
- We examine [inbound decision] jointly with [outbound decision].
- [Strategy 1] preserves risk-pooling benefits, whereas [strategy 2] contains the impact of a potential recall.
- The optimal strategy depends on the relative magnitude of [risk A] and [risk B].
- Positive correlation need not have the same effect across risks because the risks affect different parts of the supply chain.

## P2027：Behavior-Based Pricing and Peer-Induced Fairness

### Title / Abstract

标题采用“经典主题 + 新机制”：Behavior-Based Pricing + Peer-Induced Fairness。摘要用 by contrast 把本文与传统 BBP 文献的利润结论拉开。

### Introduction

引言先列 cookies、loyalty cards、Amazon、通信套餐等实践，再用消费者不公平感和政策讨论建立重要性。随后进入学术 gap：BBP 文献尚未正式纳入 fairness concerns。

### Model Section

模型段先给标准两期 Hotelling BBP，再只加入一个公平效用损失参数 $\lambda$。读者能清楚看到新 insight 来自哪里。

### Results

结果按机制链推进：二期价格和 switching、二期利润、一期价格和利润、总利润、消费者剩余、社会福利。每一步都为“公平关切反而让 BBP 更赚钱”服务。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Prior research has shown that [technology-enabled strategy] often lowers firm profits because [competitive mechanism].
- We incorporate [behavioral concern] into a standard [model] and examine whether it changes this conclusion.
- Consumers experience disutility when they pay more than other consumers for the same product.
- Fairness concerns have a direct effect on consumer switching and an indirect effect through firms' strategic price adjustments.
- Although [behavioral concern] lowers profits in the later period, it softens competition in the earlier period.
- Policies intended to protect consumers may have unintended consequences once firms optimally adjust prices.

## P1358：The Effect of Mergers in Search Markets

### Title / Abstract

标题采用 effect + market type + evidence 的 AER 结构。摘要把 negotiated rates、search process、merger variation、distributional impact 和 market power understatement 串成一条线。

### Introduction

引言先解释这个市场为什么特殊：mortgage contracts are negotiable。再用巨大 residual price dispersion 建立 puzzle，随后提出竞争是否均匀影响消费者这个问题。

### Method Section

方法段先讲制度和市场，再讲数据，再讲 choice set 和 treatment/control。这个顺序让读者先相信冲击和样本，再相信估计式。

### Results

结果先给平均效应，再说 average masks heterogeneity，然后进入 CiC 分布效应。最后用 search model 解释为什么平均效应低估 market power。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We exploit variation induced by [institutional event] that changed consumers' choice sets in some local markets but not others.
- The average effect masks important heterogeneity across the transaction price distribution.
- Consumers at the top of the price distribution are barely affected, suggesting that competition does not benefit all consumers equally.
- We use a reduced-form analysis to estimate distributional impacts and a search model to interpret the mechanism.
- In negotiated-price markets, average merger effects can understate the underlying increase in market power.
- Policies aimed at protecting vulnerable consumers should target search and negotiation frictions, not only market concentration.

## P0827：Online Advertisement Allocation Under Customer Choices and Algorithmic Fairness

### Title / Abstract

标题采用“决策对象 + 两个关键机制”：Online Advertisement Allocation 是对象，Customer Choices 和 Algorithmic Fairness 是边界。摘要按行业重要性 -> 动态分配问题 -> 公平 tension -> 原问题 intractable -> TTD/DWO 方法 -> 理论保证 -> 数值与管理价值推进。

### Introduction

引言把现实案例当作变量来源：Amazon/Facebook 对应 CPC、预算、targeting 和动态广告集合；Microsoft co-op 对应最低点击要求；广告公平争议对应公平指标。随后用 twofold challenge 组织复杂性：assortment cannibalization 与预算/点击要求/targeting rules 的可行性难题。

### Literature Review

文献综述使用“四支文献 + joint contribution”：ad allocation、algorithmic bias、service-level resource allocation、dynamic personalized assortment。每支都说明本文补了什么，不只是列作者。

### Method Section

方法写作采用 general first, special case later。先用一般 $\phi_i^j(S)$ 表示 choice model，再在 MNL 特例下给 compact convex program。这样既显得模型一般，又能证明可计算。

### Results

结果顺序是可行性 -> 可计算性 -> 可执行性 -> 渐近最优性 -> 数值表现。它先让读者相信 reformulation 合法，再展示算法有实际优势。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Motivated by [platform practice], we seek to address the following key research question: [RQ].
- Solving this problem presents a twofold challenge. First, [operational trade-off]. Second, [feasibility difficulty].
- Our framework carefully combines three ideas: [target optimization], [compact reformulation], and [online execution algorithm].
- We characterize the necessary and sufficient condition under which [target] is feasible.
- The proposed policy dynamically assigns a debt to each [target] that measures the difference between [realized outcome] and [desired target].
- Compared with [benchmark], our approach has better scalability and can [operational advantage].
- This highlights the practical applicability of our approach because [property] is desirable in [business setting].
- Our results suggest that [fairness/constraint] can be improved without significantly compromising [efficiency/revenue].

## P0052：Combined Pricing and Inventory Control under Uncertainty

### Title / Abstract

标题直接使用 combined + 两个决策 + uncertainty，典型经典 OR 风格。摘要按问题、模型、情形、结构结果、计算、数值研究推进，不使用夸张表达。

### Introduction

引言最值得学的是“实践二分 -> 文献二分”：企业中 pricing 和 inventory 分属不同部门；文献中库存模型把需求/价格外生化，定价模型把补货外生化。现实组织变化和文献空白互相支撑。

### Literature Review

文献回顾不是说 nobody has studied，而是说已有研究要么单期、要么确定性、要么需求函数太特殊、要么条件缺乏经济解释。这个写法更稳。

### Model Section

模型段每个 assumption 都服务于后续结构证明。重要技巧是：先列一般条件，再用 lemma 说明常见线性需求和凸库存成本能满足，降低读者对数学假设的抵触。

### Results

结果写法是结构定理论文标准顺序：价值函数性质 -> 最优策略结构 -> 单调性 -> 限制情形 -> 无限期/平均利润扩展 -> 计算方法。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- This paper addresses the simultaneous determination of [decision A] and [decision B] in the face of [uncertainty].
- Traditional models of [field A] assume that [key variable] is exogenous, whereas models of [field B] treat [operational decision] as prespecified.
- We characterize the structure of an optimal [joint decision] strategy under [settings].
- The optimal policy takes the form of a [simple structural policy].
- The numerical study is conducted to assess computational effort and, more importantly, to obtain qualitative insights into [policy structure].
- The benefits of [dynamic policy] are largest when [uncertainty/constraint] is most severe.

## P0957：Approximating Purchase Propensities and Reservation Prices from Broad Consumer Tracking

### Title / Abstract

标题用 Approximating 而不是 Estimating，语气克制，承认数据限制下是在近似购买倾向和保留价格。摘要用 12.99% vs 0.25% 这个强数字制造记忆点。

### Introduction

引言第一句直接从现实变化进入：消费者正在被大量 tracking and telecom companies 追踪。随后连到 targeted ads、personalized prices、GDPR/FTC 政策担忧和理论福利争议。

### Literature Review

文献对话按 behavioral targeting、personalized pricing、machine learning pricing 三支展开。每支都说明本文差异：broad web-browsing histories、revealed preference、large high-dimensional data、structural counterfactual。

### Method Section

方法段最值得学的是诚实写数据限制：没有价格变化、tier choice 不完整。然后说明用 supply-side optimal pricing condition 和 aggregate tier shares 补识别。顶刊写法不是假装数据完美，而是解释限制如何被方法处理。

### Results

结果推进顺序是 data useful -> pricing valuable -> consumer welfare -> regulation -> robustness。读者先相信预测，再看价格反事实，最后看政策。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A consumer's [behavioral trace], now readily available, may be much more useful than [traditional data] for [business decision].
- I investigate whether [new data] improve predictions of [outcome], and whether they increase profits from [strategy].
- I employ a two-step estimation procedure. First, [machine-learning prediction]. Next, [structural/economic model].
- Due to limitations of available data, not all parameters are identified without additional moment conditions.
- Simulations further reveal that incorporating [new data] substantially raises [business value].
- In principle, [policy] could improve both [firm outcome] and [consumer outcome]. In practice, however, [implementation problem] may be elusive.
- Firms may circumvent perceived unfairness by framing [price discrimination] as [discount/coupon].

## P0950：Truthful Disclosure of Information

### Title / Abstract

标题极简，直接点出 truthful disclosure。摘要先问市场是否给足披露激励，再直接给反直觉结论和政策含义，最后主动提醒模型假设限制。

### Introduction

引言用 “exactly the opposite of Akerlof” 快速定位贡献。这个写法很有力量：抓住经典文献的关键假设，反过来，看政策结论是否改变。

### Model Section

同一披露骨架给两个解释：纯私人收益和社会有价值信息。写法高效，因为读者能看到同一个模型结构如何支持不同福利判断。

### Results

Theorem 后马上解释经济含义：高质量卖家披露、披露可能是社会浪费、补贴不披露销售优于强制披露。经典理论论文不需要很多命题，但每个命题都要直接服务于政策问题。

### Limitations

结论主动列出三个限制：信号不指向最终买家匹配、质量分布外生、披露不改变市场整体信念。这种主动收边界的写法值得学。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- This article investigates whether the free market offers [agents] enough incentive to [action].
- The way this article treats [problem] is exactly the opposite of [classic paper].
- While [action] may be costly, truthful [action] is feasible, and misrepresentation is impossible.
- The article offers no support for the policy of [common intervention].
- The results should be viewed with care, however, as they depend on [special feature].
- These results are strong, but the specialized nature of the model limits their relevance for policy.

## P1459：Competition in Pricing Algorithms

### Title / Abstract

标题用 “Competition in Pricing Algorithms” 而不是 “Algorithmic Collusion”，把问题边界从合谋扩展到竞争机制本身。摘要四句分别承担 data facts、technology features、model setup、main results。

### Introduction

引言先从线上零售技术普及进入，再提出 “Do pricing algorithms lead to higher prices?”。随后把算法拆成 frequency 和 commitment 两个 primitives，全文证据、模型和政策含义都围绕这两个词展开。

### Literature Review

文献对话采用 by contrast 结构：已有算法定价文献主要讨论 learning/prediction algorithms 是否促进 collusion；本文讨论算法如何改变 price-setting game。这个差异让贡献不是换场景，而是改写理论对象。

### Model Section

模型段写法是先解释现实里的 algorithm，再定义符号。三个 special cases 对应三种现实能力：asymmetric frequency、asymmetric commitment、symmetric commitment。读者能看懂每个数学设定为什么存在。

### Results

命题后紧跟 intuition。尤其 Proposition 2 用一阶条件解释“为什么涨价”，Proposition 5 后主动说明任意价格支持结果太宽，因此再给 positive slopes、knife-edge、learning simulation 三个理由限定预期方向。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Increasingly, firms have access to better [technology], especially in [market].
- [Technology] can change [strategic behavior] by enabling firms to [action A] and [action B].
- Motivated by these facts, we introduce a model that incorporates [primitive A] and [primitive B].
- Our model allows firms to differ in [capability] and to commit to [strategy] that depends on [rival action].
- We show that [asymmetry] can fundamentally shift equilibrium behavior.
- These facts are inconsistent with the widespread assumption that firms have symmetric [decision technology].
- The model provides a supply-side explanation for [observed pattern], complementing demand-side explanations.
- Overall, our results imply that concerns about [phenomenon] are broader than [narrow mechanism].

## P1377：Theories of Cartel Stability and the Joint Executive Committee

### Title / Abstract

标题把 theories 和 historical case 并列，显示文章是理论经验检验而不是单纯案例。摘要用 “some support” 保持克制，不把证据说成 definitive。

### Introduction

引言最值得学的是重新定义 gap：Green-Porter 的核心不是 price wars should occur，而是 price wars should occur because firms follow particular trigger strategies。顶刊式贡献常来自指出“前人检验了外围，没有检验核心”。

### Method Section

方法段先承认 JEC 与经典理论原型不同，再说明如何修改理论以适配数据。这个动作比硬套模型更可信。

### Results

结果写法分层：方向是否支持、显著性多强、经济幅度是否足以维持理论均衡、是否存在替代解释。混合证据也能写得清楚。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- This article reexamines [classic setting] to assess the applicability of [theory A] and [theory B].
- The core content of the theory, however, is not just that [phenomenon] should occur, but that [mechanism].
- To evaluate this prediction within the context of [setting], it is important to note that [modification].
- The estimates provide some support for [mechanism], although [limitation].
- The results are not completely positive, in that [evidence weakness].
- I am unable to find [predicted effect], but the lack of power means that [moderate effects] cannot be ruled out.

## P0050：Optimal Dynamic Pricing of Inventories with Stochastic Demand over Finite Horizons

### Title / Abstract

标题同时交代 decision、resource、uncertainty 和 horizon：dynamic pricing、inventories、stochastic demand、finite horizons。摘要像路线图，按 problem、method、structural results、closed-form case、bounds、extensions 推进。

### Introduction

引言用 fashion retailing 和 airline seats 两个场景建立现实感，再提出 yield management 的概念混乱。作者不是直接说“我们解决动态定价”，而是解释这篇论文能澄清实践收益来自哪里。

### Model Section

模型段把价格控制转成 intensity control。这个写法很高明：现实说价格，数学说强度；控制变量变得更适合随机点过程和动态规划。

### Results

结果顺序是结构定理 -> 特殊闭式解 -> 确定性上界 -> fixed-price heuristic -> 离散价格 -> 扩展。每一步都服务于“复杂最优策略如何转化为可实施规则”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- In many industries, managers face the problem of [decision] by a deadline.
- Two key properties of this problem are [constraint A] and [constraint B].
- This generic problem arises in a variety of industries.
- Our results suggest two alternative explanations for [practice].
- Under mild assumptions, we prove that [simple policy] is nearly optimal.
- The deterministic version of the problem provides both an upper bound and insight into near-optimal policies.
- This interpretation better explains both [observed magnitude] and [observed structure] in practice.

## P0122：Feature-Based Inventory Control with Censored Demand

### Title / Abstract

标题精准压缩三件事：feature-based、inventory control、censored demand。摘要采用 Problem definition / Methodology-results / Managerial implications 的 MSOM 标准结构，并直接报告 regret rates。

### Introduction

引言从 abundant data 进入，但马上转折到 excess demand unobservable。这个 tension 比单纯“big data improves decisions”更有研究味道。

### Literature Review

文献综述按 without features、with features、online convex optimization 三块组织。每块都明确说已有文献缺什么，最后自然导出本文交叉点。

### Model Section

先给 clairvoyant optimal policy，再给算法。读者先知道全知者会怎么订货，再理解算法到底在学什么。

### Results

结果把 theorem 与业务困难对应：perishable 收敛快，nonperishable 因 overshooting 慢；DS 的改善来自 bias-variance trade-off。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The abundance of [data] brings an opportunity for decision makers to [improve decisions].
- However, when [operational constraint] makes [true outcome] unobservable, [observed data] alone cannot recover [target].
- The learning target switches from a scalar to a vector.
- Incorporating features can improve performance eventually, but limited observations in the initial periods lead to volatile learning targets.
- Ignoring [data-generating issue] cannot be compensated for by incorporating feature information.

## P0139：Joint Pricing and Inventory Management under Minimax Regret

### Title / Abstract

标题用 decision + domain + method 三段式压缩：joint pricing and inventory management 是问题，minimax regret 是方法。摘要按 First / Second / Third 分层，把 support uncertainty、Wasserstein ambiguity、data-driven comparison 分开，适合技术型 OM 论文。

### Introduction

引言先讲 marketing-operations interface，再讲经典 pricing newsvendor 的基础地位，随后指出完整需求分布已知这一假设在数据稀缺和需求波动环境下不稳。gap 不是“没人做定价库存”，而是“经典基础模型在不完整需求信息下需要 robust counterpart”。

### Literature Review

文献综述按 joint pricing-inventory、robust/data-driven approaches、minimax regret 三个知识块组织。每块最后都说本文差异，特别是“已有 minimax regret 多只做定价或库存，本文同时做 joint pricing and ordering”。

### Model Section

模型段先写 classical expected-profit benchmark，再引入 regret objective。这个顺序让读者清楚看见本文只替换了信息条件和决策准则，而不是重新发明整个 newsvendor 模型。

### Results

定理顺序是降维和解释并行：事后利润凸性 -> 给定价格的最优库存 -> 一维价格求解 -> 与 risk-free/classical/max-min 比较 -> Wasserstein 可计算结构。每个定理都回答一个自然问题，读者不会觉得数学结果是硬堆上去的。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Coordinating [decision A] and [decision B] is crucial in managing the [interface].
- Despite its parsimonious setting, [baseline model] forms the foundation for many [applications].
- The conventional modeling approach typically assumes that [strong information condition].
- However, in [volatile/scarce-data environment], [information condition] is typically unknown or ambiguous in practice.
- To address these limitations, we develop a robust and tractable approach for [problem].
- Our model can be viewed as a robust counterpart of [classic model].
- The results uncover both similarities and distinctions between [new framework] and [classical models].
- Leveraging partial distributional information can further improve the performance of [approach].

## P1649：Third-Party Software Development Kit Utilization and Mobile App Market Performance

### Title / Abstract

标题是对象 + 行为 + 结果：third-party SDK utilization 和 mobile app market performance。摘要先讲现实不确定性，再讲 boundary object theory，最后交代 DID-style 数据规模和三层发现。

### Introduction

引言用 contradictory possibilities 制造 tension：SDK 能提供外部功能，也可能带来恶意代码、app 膨胀、兼容和安全问题。这个 tension 让研究问题自然，而不是为了数据找题目。

### Literature Review

作者把已有文献界定为 platform-provided tool kits，再指出第三方 SDK 位于平台边界之外，所以不能直接套用原有平台内工具结论。这是“先承认相近文献，再指出核心边界差异”的写法。

### Method Section

方法段通过 Apple 和 Google 两个方程相减，解释 DID-style model 消掉了哪些不可观测因素。顶刊味道来自把复杂模型翻译成一个清楚识别动作：同一个 app 自己和自己跨平台比较。

### Results

结果顺序严格对应 H1-H4：总效应、类型差异、平台更新、开发者经验，然后进入机制和稳健性。这个顺序让读者不需要重新拼装主线。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Facing these contradictory possibilities, [decision makers] need to assess whether and under what conditions [practice] improves [outcome].
- Previous research has examined [inside-boundary resources], but [outside-boundary resources] may involve different roles and challenges.
- We conceptualize [artifact] as [theoretical object] and theorize [practice] as [theoretical process].
- This perspective leads us to examine how the impact varies across [object characteristics], [boundary dynamics], and [actor experience].
- Our DID-style model allows us to eliminate the influence of [unobservable common factor].
- The findings suggest that the value of [external resource] is contingent on [condition A] and [condition B].

## P1899：From Density to Destiny: Using Spatial Dimension of Sales Data for Early Prediction of New Product Success

### Title / Abstract

主标题有记忆点，副标题准确交代 data dimension 和 decision goal。摘要的转折句非常值得学：Instead of using only [aggregate time-series data], we add its [spatial dimension]，一句话完成旧方法限制和本文新意。

### Introduction

引言从一个只有三个月双周销量、却必须决定广告和促销的经理故事开始。这个真实且具体的决策困境让预测方法的必要性自然出现。

### Literature Review

文献对话精简而有方向：早期 Bass 估计不可靠，预订单和市场测试有用但不常可得，空间扩散研究又未被充分用于新品早期预测。每段都有明确的“为什么现有答案还不够”。

### Method Section

方法按机制模拟 -> 散度指标 -> 成功概率映射分层。Small-World 模拟不是为了复杂，而是给现实标签稀缺的问题提供受控校准环境；交叉熵也立刻被翻译为实际销售空间分布与基准分布的距离。

### Results

作者不回避两个误判，反而把它们转为方法边界：扩散太快时必须更早测量；区域人口异质时必须改变 baseline。顶刊方法论文常把 failure cases 变成适用条件，而不是隐藏。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- One of the main problems associated with [early decision] is the lack of sufficient [data] to enable reliable predictions.
- Instead of using only [standard data dimension], we add its [new dimension].
- This approach uses more information and requires fewer [traditional observations] to render better prediction.
- If [mechanism] is active, one can expect [observable pattern] to begin to form.
- A more rigorous approach should call for a quantitative tool that converts [pattern] into [decision probability].
- While the results look promising, caution should be exercised when applying this approach to [other conditions].
- The relevant baseline distribution should represent [external drivers] rather than assume uniformity.
