## P0128：On-Demand Meal Delivery Platforms: Operational Level Data and Research Opportunities

### Title / Abstract

标题准确交代场景、数据层级和文章目的。摘要不假装有大结果，而是说明数据覆盖什么、能支持什么研究，这是数据论文应有的克制。

### Introduction

引言先指出外卖不同于网约车：它协调消费者、餐厅、骑手三方。这一结构差异随后自然引出报价、备餐、派单和配送的端到端复杂性。

### Data Section

数据段按订单时间线而不是按 CSV 文件名讲述。每个 timestamp 都被解释为哪个阶段生成、能构造哪个运营变量、有什么内生性或观测边界。

### Research Opportunities

三个机会都遵循“现实问题 -> 数据观测结构 -> 统计/学习方法 -> 优化决策”的路径。研究议程不是泛泛的 future research，而是可马上开工的项目清单。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Different from [adjacent setting], [focal setting] coordinates activities among [multiple parties].
- These operational challenges create research opportunities.
- To support and encourage more studies on [phenomenon], we provide an operational-level data set containing [data elements].
- The variable [observed measure] captures [construct] but is truncated when [timing condition].
- This data structure can enable researchers to develop [statistical method] and use its output as input for [optimization decision].
- Rather than treat [field] as a pure routing problem, future research can examine the trade-off between [A] and [B].

## P0873：Leveraging Context: Re-Thinking Research Processes to Make Contributions to Theory

### Title / Abstract

标题通过 Leveraging Context 和 Re-Thinking Research Processes 表明文章不是简单倡导 context，而是在改变形成贡献的研究动作。摘要按问题、四步框架、贡献分类、编辑审稿含义推进。

### Introduction

引言先设置新技术现象带来的研究困境，再批评直接套理论和纯数据驱动两条不足路径，最后提出 contextualization。它证明“新情境很多”不是论文新意，如何用情境推进理论才是问题。

### Framework Section

作者用一系列对偶概念组织复杂讨论：contribution of/to theory、specific/abstract、contextual richness/overcontextualization、theory-driven/data-driven。每个对偶都说明两端极端为何不足，以及中间的可行路径。

### Reporting

情境细节应在 problem、theory 和 discussion 中持续出现，而不是只停留在 data section。只有这样，读者才能看出边界条件为什么会改变结论。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A new context alone is inadequate to constitute a novel contribution.
- The unique characteristics of the context should be preserved and leveraged in theory development.
- The contextual problem should be connected to an archetypal problem at a higher level of abstraction.
- Rather than merely applying [theory] to [context], we examine how [contextual characteristic] challenges [theoretical assumption].
- The breakdown of existing knowledge in a new context can create an opportunity for theory development.
- Contextual richness should not be treated as a threat to theory, but as a source of boundary conditions and mechanisms.

## P1655：Regulating Digital Platform Ecosystems Through Data Sharing and Data Siloing

### Title / Abstract

标题同时压入 policy levers、研究对象和结果维度。摘要不先泛泛赞美数据经济，而是先给 efficiency-versus-market-leverage tension，再给 two interventions 和一个有冲突感的结论：optimal policy preserves data cross-use but shares it.

### Introduction

引言按具体例子（Google / Apple）-> 一般制度矛盾 -> DMA 条款 -> 精确 interaction question 推进。例子并非装饰，它们分别对应数据产生市场、相邻进入市场和监管对象。

### Model Section

先用自然语言解释 data siloing 和 data sharing，再用连续参数写入模型；先解释 A 市场 demand 为什么是 data generation 的代理，再给 $\Psi_i$。模型简化时也解释 Cournot quantity 可广义理解为服务需求或 surplus extraction，主动守住外部有效性。

### Results / Policy Writing

正文采用 `Proposition -> intuition -> Insight`：数学命题报告方向与阈值，直觉段拆 direct 和 indirect effects，Insight 再翻译成政策。结论使用 `may`, `only if`, `under the model` 等限定语，避免把规范结果夸写成无条件现实事实。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The same resource that improves [service] in one market can be leveraged to create an advantage in another market.
- Two interventions may pursue the same objective, yet one can limit the resource on which the other relies.
- Rather than evaluating [policy A] and [policy B] in isolation, we examine their joint effect on [innovation and welfare].
- The direct effect is [direction], whereas an indirect effect operates through [resource / incentive channel].
- Ease of implementation should not substitute for an assessment of the underlying welfare trade-off.

## P0098：Push or Pull Supply Chains when Members' Overconfidence is Considered?

### Title / Abstract

标题用问题句把经典选择和新机制放在一起。摘要按 question、endogenous wholesale-price model、homogeneous benchmark、heterogeneous reversal、unbiased profit implication 推进，适合学习“先稳住旧结论，再讲新机制何时改变它”的结构。

### Introduction

引言用库存过剩和缺货两个反向案例引出 supply-demand mismatch，再用 Uniqlo / ZARA 解释 push/pull 的风险归属差异。行为偏差随后自然出现为“谁对需求波动更自信”。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段先用文字解释 overprecision，再给 $D_i=(1-\alpha_i)X+\alpha_i\mu$，这是行为建模写作的关键：参数必须对应可理解的心理机制。结果段区分 perceived expected profit 和 unbiased expected profit，避免把主观最优误写成真实绩效。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The conventional comparison assumes that decision makers share a common and correct belief about demand uncertainty.
- We model overprecision as a reduction in perceived variance while keeping the perceived mean unchanged.
- The benchmark result continues to hold when biases are aligned, but may reverse when the two members hold different beliefs.
- We distinguish decisions made under subjective beliefs from profits evaluated under the actual demand distribution.

## P0132：The Behavioral Promise and Pitfalls in Compensating Store Managers

### Title / Abstract

Promise and Pitfalls 是很强的标题动作：它提前告诉读者同一制度有正反两面。摘要按现实合同、理论与实验设置、主要行为偏离、by-state fairness、验证实验推进。

### Introduction

引言从 incentive compensation 的普及进入，再落到门店经理同时做 effort 和 inventory 两类决策。这样避免把文章写成泛泛的薪酬理论，而是明确运营场景中的独特性。

### Method / Results Writing

写法遵循 `rational benchmark -> observed deviation -> behavioral model -> validation experiment`。这是混合方法论文值得学习的骨架：理论先给清晰预测，实验再精准指出哪个行为环节偏离，模型解释偏离，验证实验检验机制。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A contract that strengthens incentives may simultaneously trigger behavioral responses that undermine performance.
- We first establish the rational benchmark and then examine where behavior systematically departs from it.
- The mechanism is not fairness in expectation, but fairness evaluated state by state before uncertainty is averaged out.
- The validation experiment manipulates the feature that distinguishes the proposed mechanism from alternative explanations.

## P1355：Distorted Innovation: Does the Market Get the Direction of Technology Right?

### Title / Abstract

标题用 question framing 把大问题压成一句话：Does the Market Get the Direction of Technology Right? 摘要按 problem、framework、evidence、quantification、welfare 推进，把理论和数量级评估写成一条线。

### Introduction

引言先承认 technological change 是增长引擎，再推进到 not all innovations are created equal；先承认市场擅长试错，再指出 systemic factors can distort direction。这种中间立场让论证更稳。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段把每个符号都贴到机制上：$\mu$ 是私人利润，$\tau$ 是社会外部性，$\delta$ 是路径依赖，$\sigma$ 是替代性。结果语气谨慎但明确，用 can be systematically distorted，而不是夸写成无条件结论。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Not all innovations are created equal.
- The direction of technology matters greatly as well.
- The market may be best placed to experiment, but systemic factors can distort the direction of technology.
- Equilibrium incentives can be systematically distorted when private and social rewards diverge.
- Correcting these distortions could have sizable welfare benefits.

## P1020：What Do We Know About the Science of Science in Business and Economics?

### Title / Abstract

标题把研究对象、领域和样本范围说清楚。摘要用 descriptive analysis 和 document findings，避免把描述性结果夸成因果结论。

### Data / Results Writing

数据段先解释为什么用 FT50、WOS、2008-2022、六位及以下作者。结果按 disciplines、journals、authors、universities 分层推进，每层都用 share 和 growth 指标支撑。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We conduct a descriptive analysis of [objects] based on [transparent data source].
- Our goal is to document trends in [outcome] for [levels of analysis].
- We do not suggest that higher productivity necessarily implies higher quality.
- These findings should be interpreted in light of [measurement limitation].

## P0039：Design of Patient Visit Itineraries in Tandem Systems

### Title / Abstract

标题直接压缩研究对象和系统边界：patient visit itineraries + tandem systems。摘要按 Problem definition、Methodology/results、Managerial implications 组织，适合学习 MSOM/OR 优化论文的摘要结构。

### Introduction

引言采用“经典问题 -> 文献边界 -> 现实流程超出边界”的写法。先承认 appointment scheduling 是 classic problem，再指出 single-stage 假设不能覆盖 infusion center、orthopedic clinic 等多阶段服务。

### Model / Results Writing

模型写法先定义流程时间点，再定义成本类型。命题顺序是：先证明旧性质失效，再提出 exact reformulations，再给 practice-friendly approximation 和 case study。这个顺序让读者看到从现实痛点到方法贡献再到实施价值的梯子。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A classic literature has largely focused on [simpler setting], which does not fully capture [real operational environment].
- A common practice is to [simple rule], but this approach only captures [limited linkage] and may lead to [operational loss].
- The key distinction in our setting is that [two seemingly similar quantities] have different operational meanings and cost implications.
- Due to [interdependencies], the model loses [elegant properties] often exploited in [classic models].
- We develop [exact approach] for tractability and [approximation approach] for implementation.

## P1557：碳交易背景下存在顾客环保偏好的双企业竞争策略研究

### 写作价值定位

这篇文章不是顶刊英文写作样本，主要借鉴其中文绿色竞争模型骨架。写作上可学习“政策背景 + 消费者偏好 + 企业竞争”的问题组织，但不应直接模仿其贡献表达。

### 可升级成顶刊风格的修辞动作

- Carbon trading changes abatement from a pure compliance cost into a tradable source of revenue.
- Consumer environmental preference makes abatement a demand shifter rather than merely a social responsibility action.
- The same policy may reinforce incumbent advantage or create a leapfrogging opportunity for the smaller rival.
- The direction of this effect depends on the relative magnitude of abatement cost, carbon price, and green preference.

## P0101：Reflections on the Evolution of Operations Management

### Writing Move

这篇是 reflection/commentary，不靠复杂模型，而靠清楚的样本窗口和分类维度建立可信度。写作上先承认判断性质，再给表格证据，最后提炼领域趋势。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- I provide some observations on how [field] has changed over [period].
- To prepare this comparison, I identified and classified [sample] based on [dimensions].
- These classifications are based on [method] and reflect my judgments.
- Beyond the summary statistics, there are equally dramatic changes in [research nature].
- As a field, we should expect that a contribution entails not just [theory] but also some effort to [validate/test].
- What are the new opportunities or challenges related to [field] that arise from [macro trend]?

## P0972：从双重差分法到事件研究法

### 写作价值定位

这篇是中文方法综述，不是 native English 顶刊写作核心样本。它的可学习点是“方法型文章如何制造 reader pain point”：先证明方法已经广泛使用，再指出误用会影响研究可信度，最后给出操作性建议。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- [Method] has become increasingly common in [field], yet its identifying assumptions are often misunderstood in practice.
- The estimator itself does not deliver identification; identification comes from the research design and the assumptions that make the counterfactual credible.
- The absence of differential pre-trends is supportive evidence, but it is not a direct test of the full parallel-trends assumption.
- Controls should be selected according to their causal timing, not according to whether they are correlated with the outcome.
- Researchers should begin with the institutional details of the intervention before choosing the econometric specification.

### 对 skill 的写作沉淀

方法章节适合按“直觉 -> 基础公式 -> 识别假设 -> 常见误用 -> 改进方法 -> 实践检查清单”推进。这个顺序比直接堆复杂估计量更适合读者理解，也更接近顶刊方法段落追求的透明度。

## P0144：Inventory and Production Decisions for an Assemble-to-Order System

### Title / Abstract

标题直接给出研究对象、决策和约束：inventory and production decisions、ATO system、uncertain demand、limited assembly capacity。OR/MSOM 类模型论文可以这样把边界写清楚。

### Introduction

引言先用全球化供应链和合同制造说明问题普遍性，再把 lead times 拆成 outbound logistics、inbound logistics 和 final assembly process，最后收束到 prestocking 的收益/风险权衡。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段按时间线写：time 0 预测、$t_1$ 订单确认、$t_2$ 交付。结果段按“单调性 -> 结构定理 -> 凸性 -> 算法 -> 敏感性”推进，所有命题都服务于把复杂问题变得可解。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- As supply chains become increasingly global, firms face longer lead times in [processes], which restrict their ability to respond to urgent demand.
- Prestocks can hedge against capacity shortage, but they expose the firm to overstocking risk when realized demand is low.
- Unlike prior work that assumes sufficient assembly capacity, our model explicitly captures the interaction between component lead times and limited assembly capacity.
- We establish structural properties of the optimal solution that reduce the original high-dimensional problem to a tractable convex optimization problem.
- The marginal value of reducing a component's lead time depends on whether the reduction releases binding assembly capacity.

## P0037：Competitive Two-Agent Scheduling and Its Applications

### Title / Abstract

标题用 Competitive Two-Agent Scheduling 定义问题类，再用 Applications 承诺现实连接。摘要先定义模型，再说明扩展维度和应用关系，适合理论 OR 论文。

### Introduction

引言快速进入数学框架，然后用 rescheduling 和 preventive maintenance 两个应用证明框架有现实来源。不是每篇模型论文都需要长故事；理论框架论文可以用清楚应用映射来建立 relevance。

### Model / Results Writing

结果用 complexity tables 组织。先给全局地图，再分节证明各类 theorem。这个写法适合问题变体多、贡献来自分类的论文。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We consider a scheduling environment in which two agents share a common set of machines but evaluate the schedule according to different objective functions.
- The goal is to optimize the objective of one agent while keeping the objective of the other agent below a prescribed level.
- This framework captures several practical settings, including rescheduling and scheduling subject to availability constraints.
- We provide a complexity classification for a range of problem variants and identify cases that admit polynomial-time algorithms.
- Unlike bicriteria scheduling, each job in our model is associated with only one agent-specific objective.

## P0043：The Price of Robustness

### Title / Abstract

标题把方法贡献经济化：The Price of Robustness。它没有说“a new robust linear programming formulation”，而是直接命名读者关心的 trade-off。

### Introduction

引言按“nominal model 脆弱 -> 需要 robust -> 现有 robust 太保守或太难算 -> 本文降低 price”推进。tension 简洁有力。

### Model / Results Writing

模型写法先给 nominal LP，再定义 uncertainty model，再引入 $\Gamma$。结果链条是 protection function -> linear counterpart -> probability bound -> sensitivity -> experiments，所有部分都服务于“可调、可算、有保证”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The classical paradigm assumes that the input data are precisely known.
- This approach does not take into account the influence of data uncertainty on feasibility.
- A concern with robust optimization is that it may be too conservative.
- We investigate ways to decrease what we call the price of robustness.
- The proposed formulation retains the advantages of a linear framework while offering control over the degree of conservatism.

## P1609：Faster Deliveries and Smarter Order Assignments

### Title / Abstract

标题把 Faster Deliveries 和 Smarter Order Assignments 并列，暗示“快”不是唯一目标。摘要按 platform competition、retention gap、data/IV/survival analysis、asymmetric finding、dispatch simulation 推进。

### Introduction

引言先给市场规模和低切换成本，再指出三方协调和时间敏感性，最后提出两个 research questions。它把现实重要性和算法目标错配讲得很清楚。

### Results Writing

结果段用 10 分钟早到/迟到换算 orders/day，非常值得模仿。显著性之后必须给业务量级，否则读者不知道结果大小。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Prior dispatch algorithms have largely focused on [proxy metric], while the direct impact on [business outcome] remains underexplored.
- We quantify the asymmetric effects of [positive/negative service deviations] on [future behavior].
- These estimates enable us to redesign the objective function of [operational algorithm].
- Maximizing [long-term business outcome], rather than minimizing [short-term operational metric], yields better platform performance.
- Integrating heterogeneous treatment effects into algorithmic design enables more customer-centric operations.

## P1643：Price Caps by Matching Platforms

### Title / Abstract

标题把政策工具放在中心：Price Caps by Matching Platforms，再用 Ticket Resales 界定场景。摘要采用“common justification -> paradoxical finding -> model mechanism -> welfare implication”的结构。

### Introduction

引言先铺现实事实：二级票务市场、有限供给、价格分散、政府规制退潮、平台自我规制兴起。随后制造 tension：如果 cap 总是压低交易价格，平台为何主动采用？这个问题比单纯说文献缺口更有说服力。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段按 players、timeline、matching assumptions、equilibrium concept 写。结果段先分 NC/MC/SC 三个 regime，再用 Proposition 4/5/6 合成主结论。作者给机制命名为 competition effect 和 exclusion effect，让读者在公式之外记住文章。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- A common practice is often justified as protecting [stakeholder welfare], albeit at the cost of [platform/firm profit].
- We show that this self-regulation can paradoxically harm [intended beneficiaries] while benefiting [the regulating platform].
- The key mechanism is that [policy] affects not only [direct decision variable] but also [participation decisions] and hence [competition intensity].
- A mild restriction generates [direct competitive effect], whereas a stringent restriction triggers [exclusion effect].
- These findings caution against interpreting platform self-regulation as necessarily aligned with consumer welfare.

## P0150：Deceptive signaling on globalized digital platforms

### Title / Abstract

标题用 deceptive signaling 标出机制，用 globalized digital platforms 标出场景，用 institutional hypnosis 提出概念。摘要按三类信号、数据、算法、GMM 和治理含义推进，信息密度较高。

### Introduction

引言采用“现实争议 -> 平台机制 -> 制度差异 -> 研究问题”的结构。从 Facebook/Amazon/UNCTAD 进入平台诚信，再落到 sponsored listing 不披露。

### Method / Results Writing

方法段非常重视变量构造：为什么 DHgate 适合、为什么账号是 firm、为什么美国是基准市场、如何用政策后披露训练 deceptive signal。结果段按 baseline、directionality、moderator、alternative detection、policy-after sample 层层回应质疑。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We investigate the degree and duration of the effects of [focal signal], vis-a-vis [comparison signals].
- Using transaction-level data from [platform] assembled with [data method], we develop an algorithm to detect [hard-to-observe construct].
- The effect is most pronounced when [senders] operate under [weak institution] and receivers are accustomed to [strong institution].
- After [policy disclosure], the effect of [deceptive/hidden signal] becomes insignificant, whereas [organic/reputation signal] remains effective.
- These findings reveal a dark side of [platform-mediated exchange] and offer implications for [firms, platforms, regulators].

## P0314：企业供应链风险感知与合作关系稳定性

### Title / Abstract

标题采用中文顶刊常见 X-Y 结构，直接说明研究对象。摘要覆盖背景、数据、发现、调节、渠道、进一步分析和政策含义，适合中文论文；若写英文顶刊，应把非对称核心发现提前突出。

### Introduction

引言先用全球供应链风险和政策目标建立现实重要性，再指出线性供应链视角不足，最后进入关系嵌入和结构嵌入。写中文期刊可以学它的政策连接，写英文期刊则应压缩政策背景、强化机制 tension。

### Method / Results Writing

方法段逐步交代年报下载、PDF 转 TXT、分词、词典扩展、共现窗口、CNRDS 供应链关系、网络指标。结果段按主效应、调节、渠道、提升稳定性、稳健性推进。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We shift attention from the performance consequences of [risk] to its implications for the stability of [interfirm relationships].
- The same risk perception can generate asymmetric responses because customers and suppliers occupy different positions in the transaction.
- We construct [risk measure] from annual-report text by identifying the proximity between [domain words] and [risk words].
- Relationship embeddedness and structural embeddedness jointly shape how firms respond to supply chain risk.
- The results suggest that [communication capability] and [financial flexibility] can mitigate relationship disruption under risk.

## P1619：Generative AI and Voluntary Knowledge Contribution

### Title / Abstract

标题是标准实证结构：Impact of X on Y: Evidence from A Natural Experiment on Z。摘要用 AI 正负机制开局，再交代 DID、Stack Overflow、数量/质量结果和 learning/cognitive load。

### Introduction

引言先用 90-9-1 rule 定义平台痛点，再引入 ChatGPT，随后平衡“AI 增强贡献”和“AI 带来幻觉/认知负担”两种可能。两个 RQ 组织清楚：是否影响，以及如何影响。

### Method / Results Writing

Treatment identification 写得细：检测器、ChatGPT API 相似度、0.9 阈值、用户调查准确率。结果部分先列潜在内生性问题和对应方法，再逐项验证，减少读者疑虑。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- The rapid emergence of [technology] creates both opportunities and challenges for [platform outcome].
- On the one hand, [technology] may enhance [human capability]; on the other hand, it may introduce [risk/cognitive burden].
- Leveraging [event] as a natural experiment, we employ [identification strategy] to examine [quantity and quality outcomes].
- To unpack the mechanisms, we examine the moderating roles of [cumulative usage] and [usage intensity].
- Rather than imposing a blanket ban, platforms may benefit from controlled and responsible use of [technology].

## P1906：The Effects of Personal Data Management on Competition and Welfare

### Title / Abstract

标题使用抽象但有边界的名词：personal data management、competition、welfare。它不绑定 GDPR 或某个行业，而是把文章定位成一般机制论文。摘要按“研究对象 -> 两市场模型 -> 基准发现 -> 扩展数据权利 -> 企业策略”推进，适合理论顶刊。

### Introduction

引言先用跨市场数据案例建立现实基础：智能车到保险、电商到金融、网约车到外卖、科技公司到健康服务。随后引出个性化定价争议和隐私法赋权，再给出反直觉结果。它的 tension 来自“消费者权利的政策目标”和“均衡后果可能伤害部分消费者”之间的冲突。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段采用“现实例子 + 抽象变量 + 时间线”的写法。市场 B 和市场 A 每个变量第一次出现时都有现实对应，之后再进入 Hotelling 位置、统一价格、个性化价格和删除成本。结果段每个 proposition 后都给机制解释，尤其是 opt-out 用户如何通过 uniform price 影响 opt-in 用户。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We examine how [consumer right/policy tool] affects competition in [upstream/data collection market] and [downstream/data application market].
- Consumers who exercise [right] are not randomly selected; rather, they are those for whom [firm action] would be most costly.
- The availability of [right] changes the composition of [pooled group], thereby altering firms' incentives to set [price/contract/policy].
- Although [policy] is intended to enhance individual welfare, its equilibrium effects can generate negative externalities among consumers.
- The effects of [policy portfolio] are nonmonotonic because different rights operate through different markets and competitive channels.

## P1646："Sherlocking" and Platform Information Policy

### Title / Abstract

标题把现实热词 Sherlocking 和理论对象 platform information policy 结合，既让读者立刻知道现实场景，又说明文章不是新闻评论，而是信息政策模型。摘要按 threat of future competition、seller entry、referral fee、commitment、information sharing、welfare 递进。

### Introduction

引言先承认平台 marketplace 的正面功能，再指出 hybrid model 的 player-and-referee 矛盾。Apple App Store 和 Amazon Marketplace 案例只用来建立问题，不堆砌。随后接入美国 structural separation 和 EU DMA，说明研究直接嵌入监管争论。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段按 players、market interactions、payoffs 写，适合理论论文学习。结果段先给排序关系，如 $r^{NC}\ge r^{NE}$、$r^C\le r^{NE}$，再解释为什么佣金具有承诺功能。信息设计段先用二元信号降维，再引入 obedience constraints，降低阅读难度。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Platform-run marketplaces may exploit [third-party data] to develop competing products, but the threat of future competition can deter [entry/innovation].
- We analyze the interplay between [fee structure], [entry decision], and [information policy].
- A high [fee/commission] can serve as a commitment device by raising the opportunity cost of [platform entry/imitation].
- The effectiveness of [information-sharing restriction] hinges on whether the induced signal satisfies the platform's obedience constraints.
- A regulatory ban on [platform conduct] may be counterproductive once the platform's strategic response in [fees/contracts/ranking] is taken into account.

## P1361：Growing bigger or becoming stronger

### Title / Abstract

标题用二选一对照句式 growing bigger or becoming stronger，把机制 tension 放进标题。摘要按 energy price shock、CPSC identification、revenue effect、production vs innovation mechanism、profit and energy adjustment 推进。

### Introduction

引言采用“大现实冲击 -> 中国煤炭依赖 -> CPSC 政策 -> 上游垄断传导 -> 下游适应行为”的顺序。现实背景较充分，适合政策/产业实证论文。

### Method / Results Writing

方法段先证明政策能作为价格冲击，再进入主结果。这一点值得学：如果冲击本身需要解释，不能直接跑主回归。结果段把机制拆成 production expansion 和 price/innovation 两条路径，用大量互补证据支持“变大而非变强”。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We exploit [upstream regulation] as a plausibly exogenous shock to [input price] faced by downstream firms.
- We measure exposure using predetermined input-output linkages prior to the policy shock.
- To validate the shock, we first document its effects on upstream costs, upstream prices, and downstream input costs.
- The evidence suggests that firms respond by growing bigger rather than becoming stronger.
- The findings reveal asymmetric cost transmission from a concentrated upstream sector to competitive downstream industries.

## P1657：CO2 emission regulation and generation allocation

### Title / Abstract

标题把政策对象、机制和异质性条件都放进去：CO2 emission regulation、generation allocation、heterogeneous generators。摘要按“问题 -> 方法 -> 数据 -> 效率异质性 -> 反事实结果 -> 方法价值”推进，适合结构估计 + 政策模拟文章。

### Introduction

引言先用全球减碳和中国煤电背景证明重要性，再快速进入 2021 年监管制度变化。真正的 tension 是 generation targets are stringent constraints, while emission caps are upper bounds。这个句子把现实制度冲突直接导向后文优化模型。

### Method / Counterfactual Writing

方法段的好处是公式之后立刻解释现实含义。反事实段先解释为什么只分配 generation 而不是同时分配 generation 和 emission caps，提前处理“为什么不把两个约束都放进去”的读者质疑。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Using a [unit-level] framework, we analyze how [regulation level/allocation authority] affects [cost and environmental outcomes].
- The framework incorporates [heterogeneous returns to scale] and [unobserved productivity] using a novel [fine-grained] dataset.
- Moving the regulation level from [lower unit] to [higher unit] can reduce [cost] and [emissions] by shifting [activity] toward more efficient [units].
- The distinction between [binding operational targets] and [upper-bound regulatory caps] is central to the counterfactual design.
- A more granular regulatory instrument may distort allocation when it is incompatible with higher-level operational targets.

## P1922：Designing platform incentives for balancing content creation and advertising integration

### Title / Abstract

标题用 Designing Platform Incentives 标明决策问题，用 balancing content creation and advertising integration 标明核心 trade-off。摘要先讲 creators face the challenge of balancing limited resources，再进入平台激励、努力分配和非单调结果。

### Introduction

引言用 UGC 平台规模、广告收入、平台流量分成、联盟营销、广告反感和 75%/25% 努力分配，把现实材料逐步映射到模型变量 $f$、$x$、$1-x$、$r_c$、$\theta$ 和 $b$。

### Model / Results Writing

模型段每个变量都有现实解释。结果段围绕 threshold、nonmonotonic pattern、inverted U-shaped relationship 展开。写作动作是先说直觉预期，再用 proposition 给出反转。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Creators face the challenge of balancing limited resources between [core value creation] and [commercial integration].
- We conceptualize a [creator/agent] who maximizes both [primary revenue] and [secondary commission].
- Anticipating the creator's incentive to earn [commission], the platform chooses [traffic-based incentive].
- Contrary to intuition, the relationship between [quality/capability/commission] and [platform incentive] follows a nonmonotonic pattern.
- Improvements in [commercialization capability] may reduce [core effort], ultimately lowering [platform profit] and [consumer surplus].

## P0956：Consumer Information and Discrimination

### Title / Abstract

标题先给理论对象 Consumer Information and Discrimination，再用问题句 Does the Internet Affect... 锁定现实渠道。摘要直接报告核心数字：offline minority premium、65% explained、online prices nearly equal。老派实证写法很硬朗，结果不绕。

### Introduction

引言用互联网匿名性进入 negotiated car retailing，再用既有文献冲突制造空白。结构是“技术变化 -> 机制变化 -> 旧文献争议 -> 本文数据优势”。

### Data / Results Writing

数据部分把 Price、race/gender、Autobytel match 写得很细。结果部分按逐步加控制和交互项推进，让读者看到 minority premium 如何被 observables 和 Internet channel 解释。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- To test whether [new channel] affects [equilibrium prices], we first establish that [baseline market] results in differential prices by [consumer characteristics].
- The premium declines after controlling for [observable traits], suggesting that [mechanism] explains a substantial share of the difference.
- We then interact [new channel] with [disadvantaged group] to test whether the channel disproportionately benefits those disadvantaged in negotiation.
- These results are consistent with [technology] facilitating information search and removing cues to consumers' willingness to pay.
- The people who benefit most from [tool] may be those least likely to have access to it.
## P1807：Strategic Disinformation Generation and Detection

### Title / Abstract

标题用 Strategic Disinformation Generation and Detection 同时呈现行为和治理工具，强调 generation 与 detection 的战略互动。摘要采用双问题结构：How does detection ability affect the incentive to generate disinformation? Given practical constraints, how should a detector be designed? 随后按模型、非单调发现、最优设计和反直觉结论推进。

### Introduction

引言先用 fake reviews、ad fraud、fraudulent resumes、misleading posts 和 generative AI 建立现实重要性，再把问题收窄到 true positives 与 false positives 的检测困境。它不是泛泛谈 AI 风险，而是把现实技术约束转成可建模 tension。

### Literature Review

文献综述的关键动作是指出旧文献 implicitly assumes zero false-positive rate，然后说明 allowing for false positives leads to qualitatively different insights。这个写法适合学习：不是说别人没做我的场景，而是说别人省略的现实约束会改变结论。

### Model / Results Writing

模型先讲 sender、receiver、detector，再定义 $\beta$ 和 $\alpha$。结果段给机制命名为 persuasive effect 与 dissuasive effect，把复杂均衡压缩成读者能记住的两股力量。命题写法先与 no-false-positive benchmark 对比，再讲主模型反转，逻辑推进很稳。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Previous work has focused on [one type of error] by implicitly assuming [another error] is absent.
- Allowing for [realistic constraint] leads to qualitatively different insights about [strategic behavior].
- A stronger [detector/signal] can increase [undesired behavior] when [capacity] is low because of a persuasive effect.
- By contrast, it decreases [undesired behavior] when [capacity] is high because of a dissuasive effect.
- The optimal design does not maximize [technical metric]; instead, it chooses an intermediate level that balances [benefit] and [cost].

## P0976：Competitive Bundling

### Title / Abstract

标题短到只有两个词，适合理论文：当文章能定义一个一般问题时，可以用概念化标题而不是长问题句。Abstract 的顺序是 framework -> main reversal -> contrast with duopoly -> broader contribution to valuation dispersion and price competition。

### Introduction

引言先给大量熟悉例子说明 bundling prevalent，再转入 antitrust concern 和旧文献 tension。关键写作动作是：旧文献不是被否定，而是被限定为 duopoly-based assessment。这样的语气克制但有力。

### Literature Review

文献对话按模型结构而不是按主题罗列。作者区分 duopoly Hotelling、local spatial competition、global random utility competition、auction bundling。每一类都说明为什么机制不同。

### Model / Results Writing

模型先重建 separate sales benchmark，再讨论估值分散程度，最后进入 bundling。这个顺序让读者先理解 price = market share / marginal consumer density，再接受核心反转。结果写法先给 proposition，再解释 duopoly intuition、large-n intuition 和 large-m intuition。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- Existing research often focuses on [restricted case], but going beyond this case can qualitatively change our view of [phenomenon].
- We give up the usual [modeling approach] and instead adopt [alternative framework], which can accommodate [broader set of cases].
- The intuition from [classic case] is incomplete because it ignores [mechanism].
- Whether [less dispersion / more information / stronger policy] leads to [expected outcome] depends on [where the marginal agents are located].
- The framework sheds light on [broader literature], because many activities can change [the same underlying object].

## P2006：Live-streaming selling modes on a retail platform

### Title / Abstract

标题直接说明研究对象和平台场景。Abstract 采用应用型 OM 常见顺序：现实动机 -> 模式区分 -> 博弈模型 -> 均衡变量 -> 主要发现 -> 混合模式扩展。优点是完整，缺点是 findings 较多，记忆点不够压缩。

### Introduction

引言用 CNNIC、Taobao、Amazon Live、TikTok、Walmart 等事实建立现实重要性，再拆直播与传统线上销售的差异：销售逻辑、产品展示、社交属性。这个写法适合学习“先拆现实差异，再映射模型变量”。

### Model / Results Writing

模型把员工主播和达人主播的现实差别写成定价权、努力效率、佣金、坑位费和集中/分散决策。结果部分采用 proposition + corollary + intuition，但条件较多。后续仿写时应把复杂阈值进一步命名为 demand expansion、channel cannibalization、commission burden 等机制。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- To cater to consumers' lifestyles and generate more demand, [firm] launches [new channel] in addition to [traditional channel].
- We distinguish between [mode A] and [mode B] based on the relationship between [firm] and [intermediary].
- The appropriate mode is contingent on [variable 1] and [variable 2].
- Introducing [new channel] increases sales, but its effect on [existing channel price/profit] depends on [competition intensity].
- Although [third party] exerts more effort, [mode B] does not always generate lower prices or higher sales.

## P1925：When should sellers offer rebates for consumer reviews

### Title / Abstract

标题用问题句直接指向策略决策。Abstract 按 rebate practice -> broad/targeted classification -> Bayesian social learning model -> main findings -> pricing-regime contrast 推进，适合应用型策略论文。

### Introduction

引言先强调评论数量和质量，再给返利实践，随后拆出 timing 和 content requirement 两个制度维度。这个写法适合学习“把现实工具拆成可比较制度”。

### Model / Results Writing

结果部分最值得学的是机制命名：review-induction effect、utility-enhancing effect、cost-burden effect。复杂均衡被压成三股力量，读者能记住文章主线。

### 可抽象句式和修辞动作

- We distinguish [format A] and [format B] depending on whether [incentive information] is observable before or after [decision].
- The seller's strategy is shaped by three conflicting forces: [benefit 1], [benefit 2], and [cost].
- While [broad incentive] enhances [early participation utility], it incurs wasteful spending on agents who would have acted anyway.
- Although [targeted incentive] cannot affect [initial utility], it is more effective at eliciting actions from hesitant agents.
- A change in [pricing regime] substantially alters the role of [incentive instrument].

## P1927：把平台治理问题写成 tension，而不是模型清单

- 标题动作：用“治理工具 + 激励机制 + 现实冲击”压缩主题，例如 content moderation + creator incentives + controversial content surges。
- 摘要动作：先交代奖励机制的目标，再写它诱发争议内容的副作用，随后比较两种审核制度，最后给条件型结论。
- Introduction 动作：先用真实平台制度建立现实重要性，再用 “however” 引出 unintended consequence，最后把研究问题落到平台如何平衡奖励与审核。
- Literature Review 动作：不要只说本文研究新场景，要说明把“激励”和“审核”分开研究会漏掉什么均衡反应。
- Results 写法：命题后先解释好处，再解释副作用，最后说明 trade-off 如何决定均衡。适合写作功能句：a policy designed to encourage desirable behavior may also create incentives for strategic misuse。
- Managerial Implications 写法：落到“用户结构、审核准确率、平台变现能力不同，平台应调整奖励强度和审核时点”，不要只写平台应平衡质量与风险。

## P1935：用机制标签压缩模型发现

- 标题动作：中文标题用“抢先还是等待？”制造决策张力，再用副标题限定零售商新兴技术引入时机。
- 摘要动作：先说现实背景和模型，再把发现压缩成“管理杠杆”和“时间杠杆”。这种机制标签有助于读者记住复杂命题。
- Introduction 动作：现实重要性 -> 企业为什么犹豫 -> 技术提供商为何重要 -> 两个研究问题。适合技术采用类论文。
- Model 写法：先定义策略组合，而不是先抛公式。抢先-等待、同时抢先、统一/强化/弱化服务策略，都有清楚现实对应。
- Results 写法：围绕两个杠杆讲结果：服务费改变联盟关系，垄断期改变进入节奏。未来写英文顶刊时，可把标签转成更克制的 mechanism language。
- 可复用表达功能：adoption timing, early-mover advantage, option value of waiting, exclusivity period, service design, contractual lever, strategic alignment。
## P1962：理论方法论文的 road-map 写法

- 标题动作：用短标题直接命名研究对象。适合理论方法论文，因为贡献不是某个窄行业现象，而是一个可迁移分析框架。
- Abstract 动作：problem -> tractable transformation -> equilibrium characterization -> shock propagation -> targeting implication。先交代“为什么需要多层网络”，再说明“我们如何让这个复杂问题可解”。
- Introduction 动作：先用真实多关系场景建立必要性，再把贡献压缩成几个理论结果。它的写法偏经济理论，不靠强故事冲突，而靠“旧工具处理不了新结构”来制造 tension。
- Model 写法：先定义 agents、layers、strategy、constraint、utility，再给解释性场景。这类抽象模型需要先给读者现实锚点，否则公式会显得悬空。
- Results 写法：theorem/proposition 后立刻解释 intuition 和用途，形成“可处理性 -> 唯一均衡 -> 中心性指标 -> 政策干预”的推进链。
- 可复用表达功能：A single-layer representation may obscure how interventions in one relation affect behavior in another. The key object is not centrality in any single layer, but a system-level measure that accounts for cross-layer resource allocation.
## P0960：Econometrica 式实证文章的克制推进

- 标题动作：用问题句提出机制，用副标题说明证据场景。What Drives Media Slant? 直接把读者带到 determinant 问题，而不是泛泛说 media bias。
- Abstract 动作：construct a new index -> estimate demand -> compute profit-maximizing choices -> compare actual choices -> quantify consumer vs owner roles。研究动作非常清楚。
- Introduction 动作：先写政策制度依赖的两个前提，再指出 evidence gap。它不是说监管错了，而是说关键前提缺少大样本证据。
- Method 写法：先用 death tax / estate tax 例子讲直觉，再给文本算法和公式。复杂方法需要先给现实例子。
- Results 写法：按 hypotheses 推进，先 demand，再 supply，再 alternative explanations。每张表都服务一个机制判断。
- 可复用表达功能：Existing evidence on [mechanism] is limited because previous studies relied on [manual/small-scale measure]. We construct a new index of [latent concept] that measures the similarity of [observed behavior] to [external benchmark].
## P0977：把历史事件写成可检验的均衡切换

- 标题动作：Competition and Collusion + 1955 Price War，把理论机制和具体事件绑定。
- Abstract 动作：anomaly -> conduct-change hypothesis -> differentiated-products oligopoly model -> nonnested tests -> robust conclusion。
- Introduction 动作：先用异常数据制造 tension，再说明需求解释不足，随后提出 supply-side conduct change。历史故事只做问题入口，不替代模型。
- Model 写法：先定义短期环境，即产品线固定、价格数量可调；再说明 competitive 不是 price-taking，而是 noncooperative Bertrand。
- Results 写法：围绕 escapes rejection / is rejected 推进，比单纯报告拟合优度更有结构检验感。
- 可复用表达功能：The distinct features of [event] are captured by a change in behavioral assumption rather than by changes in estimated parameters.

## P1877：把政策双面性写成克制的理论 tension

- 标题动作：用三个名词短语并列 policy、consumer mechanism、firm strategy：Privacy Regulations, Consumer Empowerment, and Versioning。理论文不必堆具体场景，标题直接呈现模型积木。
- Abstract 动作：policy aim -> firm response -> model -> monopoly result -> competition result -> extensions。先说监管想赋权消费者，再说企业会通过 versioning 回应，随后给出市场结构反转。
- Introduction 动作：现实案例不是装饰，而是变量来源。GDPR 对应 explicit opt-in consent，YouTube Premium / Meta ad-free 对应 versioning，Apple vs Google 对应 asymmetric data-driven revenue models。
- Literature Review 动作：每组文献都按“接近但缺少一个维度”定位：有的研究 privacy regulation 但不看 versioning，有的研究 versioning 但不看 privacy regulation，有的研究单一市场结构但不看垄断/竞争反转。
- Results 写法：命题后立即解释 intuition。核心句法功能是：In a monopoly, [strategic instrument] may outweigh [consumer empowerment]. In competition, however, [consumer choice] intensifies competition by creating additional deviation opportunities.
- 可复用表达功能：The impact of [policy] is nuanced and depends on [market structure], [consumer heterogeneity], and [firm characteristics].

## P1910：把经典结论放进新约束下重检

- 标题动作：Personalized Pricing in the Presence of Privacy Concerns 用 “in the presence of” 把经典机制放到新环境中。适合理论论文：不在标题夸张宣称反转，而是克制地提示“经典结论在这个约束下会变”。
- Abstract 动作：firms' incentives -> tracking technology -> innate privacy desire -> monopoly/duopoly model -> no tracking at high $\theta$ -> competition expands tracking -> regulation may backfire -> key force is commitment problem。它把多个命题压缩成一条机制链，而不是平均罗列。
- Introduction 动作：技术能力上升、商业实践出现、监管关注增强，三步建立现实重要性；然后指出旧文献忽略 privacy as intrinsic utility loss；最后用 commitment problem 给读者一个提前理解模型的钥匙。
- Literature Review 动作：每组文献都按“接近但少一个维度”定位。price discrimination 文献少 privacy-sensitive consumers；privacy regulation 文献少 endogenous tracking adoption；Hotelling 个性化定价文献少内在隐私成本。
- Results 写法：命题后立即比较 benchmark。比如 duopoly 命题后比较 monopoly，说明竞争为什么扩大 tracking。可学习的句法功能是：It is instructive to compare [new equilibrium] with [benchmark equilibrium].
- Policy 写法：不用“regulation is bad”这种过度结论，而写成 “may have the unintended consequence of...” 并反复限定条件。顶刊英文的味道来自 cautious but clear。
- 可复用表达功能：A key force that drives these results is [mechanism]. / On the surface, [result] appears to suggest [intuition]. However, a closer examination indicates [opposite interpretation]. / A regulation designed to protect [group] may have the unintended consequence of [opposite outcome].

## P1564：把成熟理论命题写成可量化实证任务

- 标题动作：Free Entry and Social Inefficiency in Radio Broadcasting 直接并列理论命题和行业场景。它告诉读者本文不是泛泛研究广播，而是用广播量化自由进入无效率。
- Abstract 动作：theory -> industry -> data -> structural primitives -> welfare magnitude -> implied external value。五句话内完成问题、方法、结果和边界。
- Introduction 动作：先给经典理论逻辑，再指出没有实证量化；随后解释数据障碍是什么，最后说明 radio broadcasting 为什么刚好适合。gap 写得具体，不是笼统说 little is known。
- Method 写法：每个方程都有福利位置。listening share function 对应需求扩张，advertising demand 对应广告主剩余，entry condition 对应固定成本。公式不是技术展示，而是反事实模拟的零件。
- Results 写法：用 numerical intuition 解释参数。比如 $\sigma=0.8$ 时，第二个电台只把听众从 10% 提到 11.3%，这比单说替代性强更有说服力。
- Limitation 写法：rough-cut estimates，但核心 business-stealing effect 稳健。作者承认数值依赖假设，同时说明为什么主要机制不容易被推翻。
- 可复用表达功能：In theory, [mechanism] can lead to [inefficiency]. We study [industry] in an attempt to quantify this inefficiency. / Relative to [benchmark], our estimates imply [magnitude]. / The observed equilibrium would be optimal if [unobserved value] were about [threshold].

## P0954：用问题标题和理论冲突写出 QJE 式清晰感

- 标题动作：Are Durable Goods Consumers Forward-Looking? 直接把论文写成一个可回答的问题；副标题 Evidence from College Textbooks 立刻给出识别场景。适合经验论文：主标题提理论行为，副标题交代数据场景。
- Abstract 动作：popular wisdom -> neoclassical prediction -> behavioral alternative -> empirical test -> result -> publisher implication。摘要不是先堆数据，而是先把两种理论直觉摆出来。
- Introduction 动作：从 textbook revision 这个日常市场现象进入，马上上升到 durable goods consumers 是否 forward-looking 的一般问题。顶刊写法的关键是“场景小，但问题大”。
- Identification 写法：作者反复强调 current price 变化有限，而 future resale value 变化更能区分行为假设。写作上不是说“我们有一个回归”，而是说“不同理论对同一个价格组成部分给出不同预测”。
- Results 写法：先给经济含义，再给估计值。比如 $\lambda$ 不只是一个系数，而是消费者把多少未来 resale value 计入当前价格的比例。
- Robustness 写法：第二个 Amazon Marketplace 证据不是机械稳健性，而是用完全不同的数据环境验证机制：二手书价格会在新版消息释放时提前调整。
- Policy/Managerial 写法：结论不是泛泛说消费者理性，而是落到出版社 revision cycle 决策。若消费者前瞻，频繁修订会降低教材资产价值，因此未必提高收入。
- 可复用表达功能：A natural test of [behavioral assumption] is to examine whether consumers respond to [future payoff component] rather than only to [current price]. / Popular wisdom suggests [myopia], whereas standard theory predicts [forward-looking response]. / The estimates imply that consumers internalize a substantial portion of [future value].

## P1913：OR 类论文把复杂系统写清楚的方式

- 标题动作：用 optimizing [system operations] and [resource management] 表示核心对象，再用 integrating [technology A], [technology B], and [strategy C] 说明本文的整合范围。
- Abstract 动作：operational challenge -> integrated framework -> network model -> profit objective -> algorithm -> numerical experiments -> conditional managerial findings。运筹论文摘要要让读者马上知道模型、算法和管理发现各是什么。
- Introduction 动作：逐层加入现实复杂性，但每一层都要对应后文模型变量。共享出行对应 rental demand，电动车对应 SOC，换电对应 swapping arcs，V2G/B2G 对应 selling arcs，动态订单对应 rolling horizon。
- Literature Review 动作：用表格按 recharging modes、decisions、methods、V2G/B2G 定位缺口。模型整合型文章尤其适合用“已有文献覆盖哪些决策组合”来写，而不是逐篇堆引用。
- Model 写法：先定义现实对象，再定义节点和弧，最后给目标函数与约束。每条弧都说明它在现实中代表什么活动、带来什么收入或成本。
- Results 写法：每个 sensitivity analysis 对应一个管理杠杆，如 reservation horizon、charging rate、fleet size、stocked batteries、Type 2 stations、energy strategy。不要只调参数，要解释参数对应什么运营决策。
- 可复用表达功能：The proposed model captures the coupling between [resource flow A] and [resource flow B]. / Each node represents a combination of [location], [time], and [state]. / Each arc corresponds to a feasible operational activity. / The value of [technology] is most pronounced when [operational constraint] is binding.

## P0176：Management Science 式政策技术评估写法

- 标题动作：Automated Enforcement and Traffic Safety 直接并列政策工具和社会结果。标题不夸大机制，正文再展开 advanced vs conventional 的技术差异。
- Abstract 动作：persistent policy challenge -> conventional enforcement limitation -> technology debate -> data and event study -> main effect -> three mechanisms -> policy implication。摘要主线非常完整。
- Introduction 动作：先用 WHO 死亡和经济损失建立重要性，再用 information asymmetry 和 negative externalities 解释传统执法为什么难，随后用 mixed evidence 制造 tension。
- Gap 写法：不是说 previous studies are limited，而是具体说它们把摄像头当成 homogeneous intervention、缺 granular intersection-level data、没有 unpack technical capabilities and driver behavior。
- Method 写法：先承认原始比较不能 causal，再列出两个识别挑战，然后引入 event study。这个顺序能让读者相信方法是被问题逼出来的。
- Robustness 写法：用表格按 empirical challenges、empirical tests、results、location 组织审稿人质疑。每个检验都对应一个威胁，而不是机械堆稳健性。
- Mechanism 写法：先用 deterrence theory 定义机制，再把事故分成 Type A / Type B / Type C 来对应不同技术能力。成熟之处在于承认机制交织，但仍设计可观察证据链。
- 可复用表达功能：The mixed evidence may stem from treating [technology] as a homogeneous intervention. / We distinguish between [advanced version] and [conventional version], which differ in [technical capabilities]. / These model-free comparisons can hardly be interpreted as causal evidence, but they reveal [identification challenges]. / The findings suggest that policymakers should evaluate not only whether to deploy [technology], but also what capabilities it provides and how users respond.

## P0975：Econometrica 式“先检验基准模型是否足够”的写法

- 标题动作：Understanding the Price Effects of the MillerCoors Joint Venture 很克制，用 understanding 表示解释机制，不在标题中直接写 collusion。适合争议政策/反垄断论文：让标题承载对象和任务，把强机制判断留给模型和证据。
- Abstract 动作：document abrupt price increases -> test Nash-Bertrand hypothesis -> quantify counterfactual price/markup gap -> relate to documentary evidence。摘要先放可观察事实，再放理论基准，再放模型拒绝与数量级。
- Introduction 动作：policy concern and theory -> empirical literature benchmark -> case facts -> structural test -> identifying assumption -> main results -> limitations。每段只推进一个必要环节。
- Method 写法：每个模型部件都对应一个识别任务。Demand model 负责 substitution patterns，supply model 负责 conduct，cost equation 负责 efficiencies，counterfactuals 负责 decomposing mechanisms。
- Results 写法：不用直接说“we prove collusion”。更顶刊的表达是 reject Nash-Bertrand, consistent with coordinated effects, may have facilitated coordination。强结论配克制动词和限定语。
- Limitation 写法：主动承认宏观冲击、模型误设和缺少正式协调理论，但说明替代解释需要不可信的大幅 ABI 成本上升。可学习动作是：不是掩盖局限，而是评估 competing explanations 的现实代价。
- 可复用表达功能：We test and reject the hypothesis that [observed outcome] can be explained by [benchmark model]. / Counterfactual simulations imply that [outcome] would have been [magnitude] under [benchmark]. / The results are consistent with [mechanism], although [caveat].

## P0955：AER 式“大事实 + 多解释 + 两步方法”的写法

- 标题动作：主标题用 Why Is... Declining? 提出大问题，副标题列出三种候选机制 Environmental Regulation, Productivity, and Trade。标题本身就是研究设计。
- Abstract 动作：large fact -> decomposition result -> quantitative model -> implicit tax estimate -> attribution。五个动作完整但不拥挤。
- Introduction 动作：先展示大事实，再公平列出三种可能解释。作者没有一开始就推销监管，而是让读者相信问题确实有 competing explanations。
- Method 顺序：先用透明 statistical decomposition 得到 model-free fact，再承认它 cannot uncover primitive forces，随后引入 stronger assumptions 的 structural model。这个顺序特别适合说服审稿人接受模型。
- Contribution 写法：贡献不是笼统说 we contribute to environmental economics，而是逐条说明：product-level granularity、overall regulatory burden/shadow price、pollution elasticity parameter、economy-wide quantitative approach。
- Results 写法：每个关键数字都给现实对照。$\\alpha=0.011$ 后，作者用 PACE 成本份额和既有 TFP 损失估计比较数量级；implicit tax doubled 后，用 Clean Air Act 具体扩张事件说明 plausibility。
- Limitation 写法：Every model is an abstraction 之后列出 markups、aggregation、technical change 等边界，但强调主要结论在多个替代解释下仍稳。
- 可复用表达功能：We begin by decomposing [aggregate change] into [channels]. While this decomposition delivers clear conclusions, it lacks the ability to uncover [primitive forces]. We complement this decomposition with [model/method] to quantify the roles of [competing explanations].

## P0966：把道德争议写成福利分解的方式

- 标题动作：Personalized Pricing and Consumer Welfare 直接把争议工具和福利对象并列。相比 Scalable Price Targeting，新标题更适合经济学和政策读者。
- Abstract 动作：controversial practice -> randomized controlled pricing field experiment -> demand model -> field validation -> firm profits -> consumer surplus -> distribution -> data-policy non-monotonicity。信息多但顺序很强。
- Introduction 动作：先用 Cambridge Analytica、GDPR、CCPA 和 CEA report 建立现实争议，再说理论没有一般结论，最后提出用企业 field experiments 解决证据缺口。
- Method 写法：机器学习不作为噱头出现，而是服务于高维异质性和定价决策。作者始终把 WLB-Lasso 连接到 posterior expected profit 和 welfare inference。
- Results 写法：不用只报系数，而是转换成企业真正关心的 price、conversion、revenue per consumer、profit gain，以及政策关心的 consumer surplus 和分配。
- Validation 写法：第二轮实验是全文可信度关键。写法上明确区分 training experiment 和 validation experiment，使模型推荐不是纸面模拟。
- Policy 写法：cautious but non-obvious。既承认 personalized pricing reduces total consumer surplus，又指出 most consumers may benefit and data restrictions may not per se improve consumer welfare。
- 可复用表达功能：The welfare implications of [practice] are not transparent because [mechanism]. / We validate our model predictions in the field using [second experiment]. / These findings suggest caution in policy debates insofar as [restriction] may not per se improve [welfare object].
## P2026：把 AI 写成 equilibrium problem 的顶刊写法

- 标题动作：Generative AI in Equilibrium 直接告诉读者本文不是普通 AI adoption，而是市场均衡文章；副标题 Evidence from a Creative Goods Marketplace 交代识别场景。
- Abstract 动作：research object -> simple model -> DID causal evidence -> substitute/crowd-out -> quality/variety/sales -> policy nuance。摘要不是结果清单，而是一条均衡逻辑链。
- Introduction 动作：先从 technological change 进入，再限定 creative goods markets，随后用 copyright/fair use 争议制造 tension。三个研究问题分别对应 substitute、crowd-out、policy evidence，使读者知道后文每个结果服务什么。
- Model 写法：模型像地图，不像炫技。作者先解释成本优势、搜索成本、variety preference、production method affects utility，再把它们压成 $\tau$ 和 $\nu$ 两条轴，生成多个可能均衡区域。
- Empirical 写法：每个识别优势都对应一个常见质疑。标签可信回应 measurement，固定价格回应 price endogeneity，同平台品牌控制组回应 counterfactual，embedding 回应内容质量和多样性不可测。
- Results 写法：先给经济结论，再读表，再回到模型区域。供给结果排除 no-entry 区域，需求结果排除市场收缩区，质量和销售率共同把市场定位到 expansion with intensified competition。
- Policy 写法：克制地写 nuance。不是说 GenAI should be banned/encouraged，而是写 benefits consumers/platform while threatening non-GenAI production，并说明 labeling/legal differentiation/niche markets 的条件。
- 可复用表达功能：We start with a simple model to highlight diverse equilibrium implications. / Our results narrow down the possible equilibria. / The evidence suggests nuance in [policy debate]. / [Technology] poses a substantial threat to [incumbent group] but is likely beneficial for [some consumers].
## P0124：用 one side of the story 写出反直觉平台实证

- 标题动作：When Persuasion Is Too Persuasive 用一个轻微反讽式 tension 把正向机制反转；副标题 An Empirical Analysis of Product Returns in Livestream E-Commerce 交代场景和 outcome。
- Abstract 动作：livestream feature -> persuasion mechanism -> live/rerun contrast -> econometric intensity model -> main effect -> moderators -> economic magnitude。摘要清楚但略拥挤，适合学习顺序，不宜学习密度。
- Introduction 动作：先承认直播电商高速增长和销售转化收益，再写 higher conversion only shows one side of the story。这个转折动作很可复用：已有研究发现 A，但 A 只覆盖前端绩效，本文加入后端绩效 B。
- Theory 写法：先区分 information effect 和 persuasion effect。前者降低不确定性，可能减少退货；后者提高预期，可能增加退货。顶刊味道来自把同一工具拆出方向相反的机制。
- Identification 写法：先具体展示 live 与 rerun 的功能差异，再引出 PSM；先举例说明评论数内生性，再引出 IV。方法不是孤零零出现，而是从场景缺陷和识别挑战里长出来。
- Results 写法：不要只写系数显著。作者把评论数一个标准差的变化翻译成退货概率增加 11.68 个百分点，并与平均退货率比较，这让结果有管理重量。
- Managerial 写法：落到绩效考核指标。不是泛泛写 firms should manage returns，而是写 streamers should be evaluated by both sales volume and return rate。
- 可复用表达功能：Higher [front-end metric] only shows one side of the story. / Without understanding [back-end outcome], our understanding of the holistic benefits of [tool] is limited. / [Tool] may amplify expectations without changing actual product experience. / Managers should integrate [front-end metric] with [back-end metric] in performance evaluation.

## P0305：把“好信号”写成边界反转的供应链实证

- 标题动作：Are Firms Voluntarily Disclosing Emissions Greener? 用疑问句承载常识张力。读者直觉会说 yes，文章用 Scope 1 和 Scope 3 的边界拆分给出 not necessarily。
- Abstract 动作：structured abstract 按 Problem definition -> Methodology -> Results -> Managerial implications 推进。结果段用 fewer internal emissions but more external emissions 形成对称反转，适合学习 ESG/供应链论文的摘要压缩方式。
- Introduction 动作：从气候变化和 SEC Scope 3 披露争议进入，而不是从文献缺口硬开头。现实政策争议负责重要性，Scope 1/3 边界负责研究问题。
- Literature Review 动作：每一类文献都用“本文补上 side effect / supply-chain boundary / firm-level evidence”定位，而不是堆引用。核心是把 disclosure literature 和 carbon leakage literature 接起来。
- Method 写法：固定效应、CEM、DID、敏感性和机制检验都对应一个读者质疑。方法不是炫技列表，而是按“主事实 -> 选择偏差 -> 动态 -> 测量误差 -> 机制 -> 政策”的审稿人疑问顺序展开。
- Results 写法：四列结果形成递进。先承认 Scope 1 更低，再问 Scope 3 是否更高，再看 total emissions，再用 ratio 支撑 outsourcing interpretation。每一列都引出下一列的问题。
- 语气动作：观察性研究用 suggest、appear、provide plausible explanation，而不是过度写 prove。尤其是 knowingly greenwashing or unknowingly lack of Scope 3 information 这一类表达，把强现实含义写得克制。
- 可复用表达功能：Voluntary disclosure may be misleading if the disclosed boundary differs from the operational boundary. / We distinguish between [internal outcome] and [external/system outcome]. / The results suggest that [positive signal] may improve [visible metric] while worsening [less visible metric]. / Regulators should evaluate not only whether firms disclose, but also what boundaries the disclosure covers.

## P1035：Operations Research 式“先解析、再推广、后决策”的写法

- 标题动作：Process Flexibility 是经典对象，Distribution-Free Approach 是方法定位，Long Chain Resilience 是新情境；标题压缩对象、方法和问题，不提前写结论。
- Abstract 动作：经典策略 -> 新风险 -> closed-form tight bound -> general moment decomposition/SDP -> capacity configuration -> 管理含义。技术信息多，但每个方法都对应一个问题。
- Introduction 动作：现实冲击 -> 企业 resilience 实践 -> 经典 long-chain 结论 -> 新风险下是否成立。gap 具体写成 performance under supply disruption remains less understood。
- Model 写法：先定义 nodes 和 edges，再定义 demand/capacity，再写 max-flow，最后定义 dedicated、full flexibility、long chain。现实对象先出现，公式随后出现。
- 理论推进：先用 $C=\mu$、MAD、Bernoulli disruption 的特例得到可解释 theorem，再用 moment decomposition 和 SDP 推广。不要一开始就把最一般框架扔给读者。
- Results 写法：每个 theorem 后立刻解释参数方向和管理含义；表格不是数值清单，而是展示 disruption probability、demand dispersion、service target 如何改变 capacity decision。
- Limitations 写法：明确承认 homogeneous infinite system、SDP scalability、异质容量和需求未处理，同时说明这些抽象是为了得到 steady-state characterization 和 clean insight。
- 可复用表达功能：We begin with a tractable special case that yields a closed-form guarantee. / We then develop a general framework to accommodate [broader settings]. / Applying the framework to [decision problem] reveals [managerial implication]. / The result highlights the importance of accounting for [risk] when making [planning decision].
## P1144：顶刊实证把“犯罪治理”写成“制度事件 + 机制排除”的方式

- 标题动作：Organized Crime and Economic Growth 把对象和结果并列，副标题 Evidence from Municipalities Infiltrated by the Mafia 交代识别场景。标题不先说政策有效，而让读者进入“组织犯罪是否影响增长”的大问题。
- Introduction 动作：从 Mafia 对地方政府和市场的渗透进入，不停留在犯罪本身，而转向 firms、entry、public procurement 和 political connections。现实重要性负责吸引读者，制度事件负责识别可信度。
- Method 写法：先承认 CCD 不是随机，再说明 matched controls、event study、pre-trends、region-year shocks 和多种稳健性如何逐层回应选择偏差。顶刊实证不是堆方法，而是每个方法对应一个识别威胁。
- Mechanism 写法：不是直接声称 Mafia 被削弱，而是先排除一般行政接管、公共支出和转移支付，再展示政治更替、政企连接下降、connected firms 受损和高风险行业进入增长。
- 可复用表达功能：The evidence is consistent with [mechanism] rather than [alternative channel]. / We distinguish between [institutional intervention] and [resource transfer]. / The results suggest that weakening [relationship network] can foster [entry/growth], especially in [affected sectors].

## P0067：Marketing Science 式“反常识短理论文”的写法

- 标题动作：The Bright Side of Supplier Encroachment 用 bright side 直接制造反常识期待。标题短，不塞机制，但暗示常识认为 encroachment 有 dark side。
- Abstract 动作：common wisdom -> contrary result -> rule out obvious explanations -> mechanism -> Pareto improvements。尤其要学 rule out obvious explanations：作者明确排除 synergies、differentiation、price discrimination，让读者相信机制不是靠现实复杂性偷来的。
- Introduction 动作：先写现实冲突和零售商不满，再说本文目的。不是从文献缺口硬开头，而是从管理者会立刻理解的渠道矛盾开头。
- Model 写法：每个假设都有“排除替代解释”的作用。同质产品排除差异化，单一市场价格排除价格歧视，需求不受侵入影响排除品牌效应，线性批发价保留双重加价。
- Results 写法：强结论配精确条件。作者不说 encroachment is good，而说 retailer benefits when $c\in(3a/(4\sqrt{2}),3a/5)$。短模型论文的学术味道来自克制和条件清楚。
- 可复用表达功能：Contrary to conventional wisdom, [action] need not harm [party]. / We demonstrate this in a model that rules out [obvious explanations]. / The gain arises because [action] induces [player] to [strategic adjustment]. / These findings suggest that managers should evaluate not only [direct competitive effect], but also [induced upstream/contractual response].
## P0962：Econometrica 式“方法痛点驱动结构模型”的写法

- 标题动作：Automobile Prices in Market Equilibrium 把 price 放进 equilibrium，而不是写成普通 demand estimation，暗示本文要解释需求、成本和企业定价共同决定的市场结果。
- Abstract 动作：develop techniques -> apply to U.S. automobiles -> estimate demand and cost parameters -> use widely available product-level data -> maintain structural equilibrium consistency。方法贡献和应用场景同时出现。
- Introduction 动作：逐个指出旧方法失败点：市场层面需求系统参数爆炸；logit 替代模式不现实；价格与未观测质量相关；aggregate data 下计算困难。每个失败点对应后文一个方法模块。
- Method 写法：先给一般效用，再讲 logit 的局限，再引入 random coefficients；先给边际成本，再给企业利润，再推一阶条件和 markup。读者能看到技术不是凭空出现，而是被识别和反事实需求逼出来。
- Results 写法：不只报参数，而把参数翻译成 elasticities、cross-price substitution、outside substitution、markups 和 variable profits。结构模型必须把估计参数转成经济对象，否则读者无法判断可信度。
- Limitation 写法：主动承认反事实是 conditional analysis，产品特征内生、micro data、cost data、dynamic demand 和 used car market 都是未来方向。顶刊方法论文不会夸大一个静态模型能解决所有政策问题。
- 可复用表达功能：A familiar alternative is to posit [simple system], but this requires [implausible restriction]. / We solve the aggregation problem via [method] and the simultaneity problem via [method]. / The estimates can be used for [policy analysis], conditional on [fixed primitives].
## P0055：MOR 式“现实信息缺陷 -> 算法保证”的写法

- 标题动作：Non-Parametric Asymptotic Analysis 说明方法，Inventory Planning 说明对象，Censored Demand 说明核心难点。标题把“为什么难”写进去了。
- Abstract 动作：unknown distribution + unobservable lost sales -> adaptive policies -> newsvendor benchmark -> $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate。摘要不写业务口号，直接交代信息结构、算法和理论保证。
- Introduction 动作：先讲经典库存理论依赖 demand distribution，再讲现实中 distribution family 和 parameters 都不清楚，最后加入 censored demand 作为更强困难。
- Method 写法：算法先用现实语言解释：缺货往上调，剩货往下调。然后才写投影随机梯度下降和 theorem。算法论文也要先让读者理解动作。
- Proof 写法：把证明架构拆成两块。易腐库存由 online convex optimization 直接控制；非易腐库存新增 carry-over difficulty，再用 queueing process 处理。这种 proof roadmap 很值得学。
- 可复用表达功能：The key observation is that [censored signal] can be used to construct an unbiased estimate of [subgradient]. / We compare our policy against the benchmark that would have been achieved if [full information] were available. / The main difficulty in [extension] is [path dependence], which we handle by [auxiliary process].
## P0989：QJE 式“制度细节长成识别设计”的写法

- 标题动作：Watering Down Environmental Regulation in China 用 water 双关，既指水污染，又暗示政策执行被稀释或扭曲。短标题承载了机制感。
- Abstract 动作：spatial RD -> upstream/downstream incentive -> TFP and COD effects -> nonpolluting placebo -> post-promotion timing -> career-driven leaders -> aggregate cost。摘要是完整证据链，不只是结果清单。
- Introduction 动作：发展中国家污染 trade-off -> 中国中央-地方制度 -> imperfect monitoring -> local officials optimize readings -> spatial inequality。现实问题、政治经济机制和识别设计在引言中已经合成。
- Method 写法：先把监测站制度讲清楚，再写 RD 方程。读者先相信 discontinuity 从制度中来，公式才自然。
- Results 写法：按审稿人疑问排序：主效应、非污染 placebo、2003 timing、企业内变化、排放机制、政治激励、自动监测站、经济成本。
- Policy 写法：不简单说环保监管好或坏，而说 imperfect monitoring distorts decentralized implementation。顶刊政策含义通常是 nuance，而不是站队。
- 可复用表达功能：We exploit [institutional feature] that creates discontinuous incentives around [boundary]. / The effect appears only after [incentive change], only for [affected group], and only for [targeted outcome]. / These patterns are consistent with [mechanism] rather than [alternative explanation].
## P1553：综述写作的分类框架与局限

- 标题动作：直接点明对象“不对称信息下的供应链管理”和任务“综述与展望”，适合中文综述，但缺少顶刊 review 的理论框架感。
- Abstract 动作：研究现状描述 -> 四类文献分析 -> 未来展望。信息完整，但主线偏概括。
- Literature Review 动作：按信息传递、协调机制、渠道结构、风险管理四类组织，适合作为入门地图。
- 可改进写法：若要顶刊化，应把每类文献写成 private information、player timing、mechanism、equilibrium distortion、coordination device、open question，而不是主要列举作者和结论。
- 可复用表达功能：The literature can be organized by [information type], [transmission mechanism], [coordination contract], and [channel structure]. / Future work should move from one-sided private information to bilateral or multilateral information asymmetry.
## P1145：QJE 式“旧争论推进到再配置机制”的写法

- 标题动作：Reallocation Effects of the Minimum Wage 直接把新机制放在标题，而不是写 Minimum Wage Effects。读者一眼知道本文不是重复就业效应争论。
- Abstract 动作：policy exposure -> wages/employment -> worker reallocation -> contribution share -> regional establishment quality。摘要既给主效应，又给机制和量化贡献。
- Introduction 动作：先承认最低工资就业争议，再提出本文的新问题 how the labor market absorbs wage increases。这个转折把旧文献往前推进一步。
- Method 写法：作者主动解释低薪工人天然有更高工资增长和更低就业稳定性，因此用 prepolicy changes 校正。顶刊实证的写法是先替审稿人提出威胁，再展示设计如何回应。
- Results 写法：先证明工资涨且就业不降，再进入 reallocation。机制结果依赖主效应可信，因此顺序不能反。
- 可复用表达功能：We ask how the market absorbs [policy-induced increase]. / The policy did not merely affect [average outcome]; it changed the allocation of [agents] across [organizations]. / This upgrading accounts for [share] of the induced increase in [outcome].

## P0033：Management Science 式“现实随机流 -> 可解优化模型”的写法

- 标题动作：Managing Appointment-Based Services in the Presence of Walk-In Customers 把管理对象和关键扰动同时写清楚。in the presence of 很适合表达“旧问题放进新环境后会变”。
- Abstract 动作：prevalence/significance -> lack of planning tools -> first optimization model -> structural/algorithmic contribution -> numerical and case-study savings。摘要不是算法清单，而是完整交代为什么需要模型、模型能处理什么、实际能省多少。
- Introduction 动作：先从银行、酒店、餐厅、美容院、技术支持等普遍场景进入，再聚焦医疗门诊，用医生访谈制造真实 tension。OR 论文也需要现实声音，不要一上来就写变量。
- Data 写法：数据分析不是独立炫技，而是为模型假设服务。作者先证明 walk-in 到达可能是 provider-specific 和 zero-inflated，后文“一般到达过程”的建模选择才自然。
- Model 写法：先定义 session、slot、walk-in vector、schedule vector、成本项，再给公式。每个符号都有现实对象，读者不会觉得数学悬空。
- Proposition 写法：每条命题都连接一个功能。multimodularity 说明局部搜索可全局最优；无超排说明 benchmark 的结构；no-show 后结构复杂说明新模型必要。
- Numerical 写法：按读者问题组织实验：算法快不快，排班形状如何变，有没有简单规则，真实数据能省多少。不要只做参数敏感性列表。
- Managerial 语气：Scheduling is an effective way to counter some, but not all, of the negative impact。顶刊味道来自“有用但克制”，不是把模型说成万能。
- 可复用表达功能：Despite the prevalence and significance of [phenomenon], we know relatively little about how to [operational decision]. / Motivated by these empirical findings, we develop [model] that can accommodate [general process]. / [Phenomenon A] introduces a new source of uncertainty and cannot be viewed as a simple solution to [phenomenon B]. / The optimal schedules are fundamentally different from those identified in settings without [key phenomenon].

## P0041：OR 理论文“简单规则 -> 理论保证”的写法

- 标题动作：Constant Job-Allowance Policies 点出简单政策，Appointment Scheduling 点出应用场景，Asymptotic Optimality and Numerical Analysis 点出理论和有限样本验证。
- Abstract 动作：practice policy -> previous dome-shape finding -> explicit performance gap -> asymptotic optimality -> extensions -> numerical near-optimality。理论论文摘要要让读者看到“结论、条件、证据”三件事。
- Introduction 动作：先讲等待/空闲 trade-off，再讲 dome shape 已知但不可直接实施，最后讲 plateau policy 被医院真实采用。gap 很具体：知道形状不等于知道可执行政策。
- Literature Review 动作：把旧文献定位为 numerical characterization，把本文定位为 theoretical justification。不是说旧文献错，而是说旧文献还缺一层 guarantee。
- Model 写法：先给 Lindley recursion，再给总成本。公式少但每个都承担系统动态，不为了复杂而复杂。
- Theory 写法：定理前先铺 newsvendor benchmark、random walk、D/G/1 queue。读者先知道证明对象为什么出现，再读 theorem。
- Proof Roadmap：先改写成本差，再控制误差项，再用最优成本下界转成相对 gap。未来写理论证明时应主动给这种路线图。
- Numerical 写法：数值实验不是重复定理，而是回答渐近结论在小规模是否仍有意义。
- 可复用表达功能：A natural question is how well [simple policy] performs in comparison to more sophisticated policies. / We provide a theoretical justification for [widely used practice] by proving [performance guarantee]. / Although [structural property] has been observed numerically, it does not directly lead to an implementable policy. / Our analysis validates the practical appeal of [simple rule] and clarifies when it is most desirable.

## P1376：结构 IO 把理论模型翻译成计量方程的写法

- 标题动作：A Study of Cartel Stability 直接命名大问题，副标题 The Joint Economic Committee 1880-1886 交代历史场景。标题克制，不提前宣称结论。
- Abstract 动作：data -> proposition -> equilibrium model -> estimating equations -> alternative hypothesis。最关键写法是 theory determines the estimating equations and stochastic structure。
- Introduction 动作：先承认 collusion identification 没有 smoking gun 很难，再综述 conjectural variation、结构冲击、非嵌套检验，最后引出 Green-Porter dynamic model。文献综述承担“为什么需要新识别策略”的功能。
- Institutional Section 动作：JEC 历史不是背景装饰，而是逐条服务于模型假设：同质产品、不完美监测、市场份额协议、进入处理、湖运竞争。
- Model 写法：先解释 collusive、Bertrand reversion、Cournot 的经济含义，再推导 aggregate supply relationship。理论到方程的翻译链条清楚。
- Results 写法：不仅报显著性，还转成价格高 66%、数量低 33%、收入高 11%。结构模型结果要落到经济量级。
- Limitation 语气：作者承认湖运价格缺失、需求残差解释力弱、触发原因难完全确认。强结论和边界说明并存。
- 可复用表达功能：The problem of distinguishing empirically between [behavior A] and [behavior B], in the absence of a smoking gun, is a difficult one. / This hypothesis is tested against the alternative that no switch took place. / The results are consistent with [mechanism], although the precise trigger remains difficult to identify.
## P1559：JPE 式“不可观察机制 -> 可观察门槛”的写法

- 标题动作：Entry and Competition in Concentrated Markets 非常克制，只交代对象和问题，不提前宣称“几家企业足够竞争”。这种标题适合方法框架型 IO 论文。
- Abstract 动作：empirical framework -> theoretical entry model -> five industries -> relationship among firm counts, market size, and competition -> main result -> price validation。摘要先交代方法框架，再给实证应用和核心发现。
- Introduction 动作：先摆出 contestable markets 与 entry barriers 的理论分歧，再指出要区分这些理论需要更细的 entry model。随后引出关键困难：不观察 price-cost margins。贡献自然出现为“没有 margins 时也能估计 entry effects”。
- Theory 写法：每个公式都翻译成现实对象。$S_1$ 是垄断者不亏损所需市场规模，$s_N$ 是每家企业对应市场规模，threshold ratio 是 conduct 变化速度。符号不悬空，这是顶刊结构模型写作的底线。
- Empirical 写法：ordered probit 不是技术名词展示，而是利润门槛概率化。作者先解释观察到 N 家企业意味着什么，再引入 likelihood。
- Robustness 写法：按威胁组织，而不是按表格组织。固定成本、市场规模定义、变量利润、市场隔离，每一项都回答一个具体质疑。
- Conclusion 写法：强调两点优势：不需要价格/数量数据；让隐含假设显性化。同时承认市场重叠和进入退出动态尚未处理。
- 可复用表达功能：When [object] is not observed, [equilibrium outcome] can still provide information about [unobserved primitive]. / We develop the idea of [threshold object], a measure of [minimum condition required for equilibrium outcome]. / Ratios of [thresholds] provide scale-free measures of how [conduct] changes with [market structure]. / These estimates should be interpreted under maintained hypotheses about [costs], [market boundaries], and [product homogeneity].
## P1567：AER 式“生活迁移事实 -> 动态需求资本”的写法

- 标题动作：The Evolution of Brand Preferences 是理论对象，Evidence from Consumer Migration 是识别场景。标题同时回答“研究什么”和“靠什么识别”。
- Abstract 动作：new data -> life histories and purchases -> migration variation -> causal effect of past experiences -> persistence magnitude -> 40 percent geographic variation -> entry barriers and first-mover advantage。每一句都是信息增量。
- Introduction 动作：先列出品牌溢价的生活事实，再列出理论渠道 habit、switching costs、quality beliefs、advertising、peer observation、childhood。随后指出旧研究多是短期效应，本文看 long-run evolution。
- Data 写法：每个数据源都有功能，不做流水账。Homescan 负责当前购买，life histories 负责迁移，CCA 负责历史份额假设，IRI 负责价格反事实，TNS 负责广告机制。
- Descriptive Evidence 写法：先让读者看到迁移者的 immediate jump 和 slow convergence，再引入模型。这种顺序让结构模型像解释事实的镜头，而不是硬塞进去的技术。
- Model 写法：作者主动称模型是 a lens through which to interpret these results，并承认 top-two brands、separability、myopic consumers 等限制。顶刊写作里的克制感来自“模型有用，但不是全世界”。
- Identification 写法：单独开节 Evidence on Identifying Assumptions。不是把假设藏起来，而是把迁移选择和历史份额两个核心风险放到台面上逐个回应。
- Counterfactual 写法：把参数翻译成进入壁垒：后进入者要多大折扣、持续几年才能追平。这比只报告 $\alpha$ 和 $\delta$ 更有经济学味道。
- 可复用表达功能：Variation in [history] allows us to isolate the effect of [past experience], holding constant [current environment]. / We introduce a simple model as a lens through which to interpret these patterns. / Counterfactuals suggest that [accumulated stock] can create durable advantages for incumbent firms. / The evidence is consistent with [mechanism], although we cannot interpret these relationships as causal.
## P1994：把变量关系写成“公共成本”的应用型实证写法

- 标题动作：The public cost of extreme climate 把变量关系提升为概念问题；Empirical evidence from China's Urban Investment bonds 交代证据窗口。比 “High temperature and UIB spreads” 更有研究问题感。
- Abstract 动作：public cost needs to be identified -> temperature bins and bond-level data -> high temperatures raise spreads -> guarantee capacity and financing demand mechanisms -> investor attention, adaptation, heterogeneity, long-term prediction。摘要不是结果清单，而是一条“公共成本被市场定价”的链条。
- Introduction 动作：从“私人部门已有大量证据”转向“公共部门被忽略”，再把 municipal bonds 写成观察财政压力的金融窗口。可复用功能句是：Existing research has documented how [shock] affects [well-studied actors], yet much less is known about its consequences for [neglected actor].
- Institutional background 动作：制度背景服务识别，不是背景装饰。作者解释为什么正式地方债不够市场化，而城投债更能反映地方政府行为和市场定价。
- Method 写法：每个变量都要有现实含义。温度 bins 对应非线性尾部风险；债券利差对应市场风险补偿；LGFV 固定效应对应融资平台长期差异。
- Results 写法：先报告经济量级，再解释“这意味着风险被资本化”。不要只说显著，要把 2.6495 个基点换成相对平均利差的现实大小。
- Mechanism 写法：主效应后分两条机制线，一条是能力下降，一条是需求上升。这个双线结构适合很多供应链和平台研究：冲击既可能削弱履约/担保能力，也可能增加融资/服务/补贴需求。
- Caution 写法：这篇不是顶刊英文模仿的最高样本，部分稳健性略机械。吸收它的结构动作和方法动作即可，写作上要更克制地把每个稳健性检验绑定到具体威胁。
- 可复用表达功能：Financial prices provide a window to identify otherwise hard-to-observe costs. / The effect of [risk] is capitalized into [market price]. / The mechanism operates through both weakened [capacity] and increased [demand]. / Adaptation measures can mitigate the pricing impact of [risk].
## P1999：应用型实证中“成本收益缺口”的写作套路

- 标题动作：Valuing high temperature's fiscal costs 直接说明任务是估值，不是泛泛检验影响；Evidence from China 交代经验场景。
- Abstract 动作：policy need -> neglected fiscal costs -> county fiscal data and weather data -> expenditure and balance pressure -> social and health spending mechanism -> decision framework。顺序清楚，但英文不宜作为顶刊风格最高模板。
- Introduction 动作：把 gap 写成政策评估偏误。忽略气候对财政部门的损失，会扭曲减排和适应政策的成本收益分析。这比简单说 literature neglects public sector 更有力。
- Method 动作：先定义财政结果对象，再给温度 bins。读者会看到作者不是为了跑天气回归，而是为了拆财政可持续性。
- Results 动作：收入不显著也要解释。高水平写作不只强调显著项，还要说明为什么某些预期结果不明显，以及这对机制意味着什么。
- Mechanism 动作：用预算科目做机制。社会、医疗、农业支出对应公共需求；流转税和企业所得税占比对应税基变化。机制变量来自制度账本，比泛泛中介更可信。
- Policy 动作：用转移支付和碳交易把结论落到“谁改变什么政策工具”。不要只说地方政府应重视气候风险，要说上级政府可通过转移支付分担区域气候财政风险。
- 可复用表达功能：Ignoring [hidden cost] may distort cost-benefit analyses of [policy]. / We decompose [aggregate fiscal outcome] into [revenue side] and [expenditure side]. / The results suggest that [shock] operates primarily through [budget category]. / [Policy instrument] can mitigate the fiscal pressure induced by [shock].
## P1975：方法基础设施论文的“demystify -> operationalize -> caution”写法

- 标题动作：AI Agents for Economic Research 直接把新对象和应用场景放在一起。适合工具或方法本身足够新、读者首先需要知道“这是什么、用于哪里”的文章。
- Abstract 动作：objective -> definition -> accessibility goal -> examples -> implementation framework -> full research process。先 demystify，再 operationalize，避免只写“AI 很重要”。
- Introduction 动作：用技术演化制造 tension。作者不是泛泛说 AI develops rapidly，而是写出从 chatbots 到 reasoning models 再到 agents 的 architectural advance，说明研究流程为什么因此改变。
- Context 写法：续写热点领域时，要明确“新阶段的新问题”。本文相对早期 GenAI for economics 文献的推进，是从 LLM use cases 转向 agentic workflow。
- Method 写法：每个工具例子都绑定一个研究痛点。Deep Research 对应文献综述，coding agents 对应计量工具，FRED agent 对应经济数据检索，LangGraph 对应复杂工作流。
- Claim 语气：强能力配强边界。可学习表达功能是：agents can autonomously conduct many tasks, but they compile existing knowledge rather than generating novel insights。写 AI 论文尤其需要这种 cautious but useful 的语气。
- Conclusion 动作：回到人类研究者的不可替代能力。AI agents 是 superb research assistants but not yet independent researchers；最后落到 creative problem formulation、ethical reasoning 和 social welfare judgment。
## P1409：Management Science 式“现实重要 + 理论 ambiguity + 高透明识别场景 + 机制链条”写法

- 标题动作：主标题用问题句 Does Employing Skilled Immigrants Enhance Competitive Performance? 抛出管理者关心的一般问题；副标题 Evidence from European Football Clubs 交代具体证据窗口。
- Abstract 动作：research question -> setting transparency -> rule-based IV -> LATE main effect -> talent mechanism -> coordination/strategic variety mechanism -> novelty。摘要重点不是堆表格，而是把识别和机制连成一条线。
- Introduction 动作：先写现实压力，移民监管更复杂、人才竞争更激烈；再写理论 ambiguity，human capital 认为可能更好，assimilation 和 diversity friction 认为可能更差。这样研究问题自然出现。
- Literature Review 动作：文献综述为机制服务，而不是堆引用。作者把移民、human capital、多元化、组织设计和 strategic variety 文献接起来，目的是引出“人才如何被组织部署”。
- Method 写法：先承认 OLS 的 omitted-variable problem，再引出规则变化 IV。顶刊式实证写作往往先替审稿人提出威胁，再说明研究设计如何回应。
- Mechanism 写法：机制不是一张中介表，而是三步推进：talent -> strategic variety -> coordinator role。每一步都回答上一层还没解释清楚的问题。
- Limitation 语气：主动承认 football setting 不完全可推广，LATE may be an upper bound。克制语气增强可信度，不削弱文章贡献。
- 可复用表达功能：Whether [input] improves [performance] is theoretically ambiguous because [positive mechanism] and [negative mechanism]. / We exploit [rule changes] that alter organizations' ability to employ [input]. / The findings suggest that [input] matters not only because of individual quality, but also because it changes how work is coordinated.
## P1484：经典方法论文的“先讲因果难题，再讲工具”的写法

- 标题动作：Propensity Score-Matching Methods for Nonexperimental Causal Studies 直接说明方法和应用场景。方法论文标题重在清晰，不重在修饰。
- Introduction 动作：先讲非实验因果推断的根本困难：treated 和 control 可能因 self-selection 不可比；再讲 matching；最后才讲 propensity score。顺序是 problem -> old solution -> dimensionality problem -> proposed method。
- Theory 写法：每个 proposition 都回答一个直观问题。randomization 为什么消除偏差，covariate matching 何时成立，propensity score 为什么能降维。公式服务于读者理解，不是为了显得技术。
- Data/Validation 写法：先展示 full CPS/PSID 和 NSW treated group 差异巨大，再展示匹配如何筛出可比 controls。让读者先看到 naive comparison 为什么错，再接受方法。
- Results 写法：不只报告 treatment effect，还报告 matched sample characteristics。方法论文尤其要展示诊断过程，而不只是最终估计。
- Conclusion 语气：PSM 有用，但 estimate quality is limited by comparison-group quality。好的方法论文不会把方法写成万能，而会说明适用条件和失败边界。
- 可复用表达功能：The process of searching for a comparable subset is itself revealing. / The method pares the large pool of potential controls down to relevant comparisons without using outcome information. / The quality of the estimate is limited by the quality of the comparison group.

## P1555：中文模型论文“现实责任条款 -> 主从博弈”的写法

- 标题动作：标题直接压缩了场景、机制和方法对象：“随机需求”“库存责任期”“装配系统”“准时供货模型”。优点是信息密度高，缺点是缺少英文顶刊标题常见的 tension 感。
- 引言动作：从 JIT 的现实价值进入，再转到库存责任归属争议，最后提出责任期过长可能诱发供应商延迟供货。这个结构适合中文运筹模型论文。
- 模型写法：先定义库存责任期的现实含义，再说明制造商和供应商利益方向相反，然后进入 Stackelberg 顺序。公式服务于责任边界机制，而不是单纯堆数学。
- 结果写法：核心不是“求得最优解”，而是把最优解翻译成管理判断：制造商不应设置过长责任期，多供应商不同步会造成系统损失。
- 英文顶刊改写方向：可把 tension 写成 a contractual instrument that shifts inventory risk may also distort delivery incentives。贡献句可以写成 responsibility-boundary design as an incentive instrument in JIT assembly systems。

## P1660：QJE 式“现实担忧 -> 三个实证挑战 -> 对应识别设计”的写法

- 标题动作：Digital Distractions 给出现实对象，Peer Influence 给出机制，Academic and Labor Market Outcomes 给出长短期结果。标题没有先宣布结论，而是把问题空间压缩清楚。
- Abstract 动作：concerns -> estimates -> data -> identification -> contagious behavior -> academic/wage effects -> peer total effect -> policy simulation -> mechanism。摘要像一条完整证据链，不是表格清单。
- Introduction 动作：先用全球现实和政策行动建立重要性，再用 rigorous evidence remains limited 指出缺口。随后用 three key challenges 组织方法，分别对应 rare linked data、endogeneity、peer effects/reflection problem。
- Method 写法：先替审稿人提出威胁，再给解决方案。随机宿舍、原神上线、游戏限制政策不是炫技，而是逐个回应 peer selection、own app endogeneity、peer behavior identification。
- Results 写法：结果都转成经济量级。GPA 用 within-cohort-major standard deviation，工资用百分比，政策模拟用初始工资提升 0.9%。这比只报系数更有顶刊味道。
- Mechanism 写法：机制不是单一中介表，而是 GPS、睡眠、问卷三类证据互相补强。每类证据都回答“为什么 app usage 会伤害人力资本”。
- 可复用表达功能：Empirical research on [X] faces three key challenges. / To overcome this obstacle, we leverage [linked data]. / To address endogeneity, we exploit [external shock]. / Disentangling these effects is crucial because [policy evaluation reason]. / The findings suggest that [private behavior] generates [externality].

## P1317：中文经济学“现实场景迫使方法改造”的写法

- 标题动作：主标题交代两个估计对象，副标题说明制度应用。信息明确，但英文顶刊式 tension 可进一步强化为“service production differs from manufacturing”。
- Introduction 动作：先讲医疗供需矛盾，再讲资源配置需要生产函数和需求函数，随后指出工业企业方法不适用于医院。方法创新从现实差异中自然长出来。
- Method 写法：每个技术处理都对应一个现实问题：要素利用率回应服务无法库存；病情调整回应医疗产出异质；价格指数回应平均支出混合价格和病情；IV 回应等级内生。
- Results 写法：不只报告估计值，还把估计结果用于制度解释：小病大医和冲击反应灵活性。这让方法结果有政策落点。
- Limitation 写法：作者承认生产率黑箱、等级内生性和供需割裂，提出未来可做动态升降级和供需联立均衡模型。这个克制值得学。
- 可复用表达功能：Standard estimators implicitly assume [condition], which is problematic in [service setting]. / We adapt [method] to account for [utilization/output heterogeneity]. / The institutional rating system improves information provision but may distort demand allocation. / A future model should jointly determine supply and demand responses.

## P0137：方法论文如何把复杂算法写成清楚主线

- 论文：Brice, Jiang, and Wan (2011), *A Cluster-Based Context-Tree Model for Multivariate Data Streams with Applications to Anomaly Detection*。
- 标题动作：用“方法 + 数据对象 + 应用任务”压缩论文，例如 cluster-based context-tree model 是方法，multivariate data streams 是对象，anomaly detection 是应用。
- 摘要动作：先写 many applications require X，再写 existing models suffer from Y，再写 this paper proposes Z，最后用多场景实验说明 effectiveness。这个结构适合算法/运筹方法论文。
- 引言动作：用 motivating application 进入，不直接堆技术。本文先讲 video broadcasting systems 的异常监控，再把问题抽象为 context-dependent data streams 的建模瓶颈。
- 文献综述动作：按“问题部件”组织文献，而不是按年份堆砌。data stream clustering 解决动态更新，categorical clustering 解决类别聚类，CT/VLMM 解决上下文依赖，本文贡献就是桥接三者。
- 方法章节动作：每个公式都要交代 pipeline 角色。$P(x\mid s)$ 用于预测下一个状态，$\Delta I$ 用于决定 cluster 合并，$K(P_1,P_2)$ 用于判断不同时间窗口是否异常。
- 结果章节动作：验证层次从 motivating real data 到 benchmark comparison，再到 Monte Carlo sensitivity。这样能同时证明现实相关性、相对优势和机制稳健性。
- 结论动作：方法论文可以主动承认边界，例如大样本需求、离散化损失、参数调试。克制地写局限会增强可信度。

## P1522：RFS 实证文章的“怀疑驱动型”写法

- 论文：Chemmanur, Loutskina, and Tian (2014), *Corporate Venture Capital, Value Creation, and Innovation*。
- 引言主线：先写创新组织形式的现实争议，再把 CVC 写成 hybrid model，随后用正反机制制造 tension。CVC 可能因长期视角、战略目标和技术知识更好，也可能因内部资本市场和战略掠夺更差。
- 摘要动作：we analyze how A differs from B；we find X；baseline results continue to hold in PSM and DiD；we present evidence consistent with mechanisms。结果、识别和机制一次交代。
- 实证章节动作：每一步都回应一个怀疑。OLS 回答是否有关联；PSM 回答可观测选择；全量样本 DiD 回答 IPO 幸存者偏差和投资前趋势；投资模式 probit 回答是否只投成熟企业；机制检验回答为什么。
- 结果写法：不要只写显著性，要把 log 系数翻译成现实量级，例如 0.269 对应约 26.9% more patents。
- 机制写法：机制不能只靠故事，要有理论来源和变量构造。technological fit 对应产业知识机制，failure tolerance 对应 Manso 创新激励理论。
- 语气：作者承认 cannot completely rule out selection effect，但说多项证据说明结果 unlikely entirely driven by selection。这种克制但有力的语气很适合顶刊实证写作。

## P1505：复杂战略选择论文先画“完整菜单”再定位贡献

- 论文：Ceccagnoli, Higgins, and Kang (2015), *Corporate Venture Capital as a Real Option in the Markets for Technology*。
- 标题动作：把理论镜头嵌入标题，CVC as a Real Option 直接告诉读者本文不是普通 CVC 文章，而是期权理论文章。
- 引言动作：先用行业实践说明 CVC 的 future technology window 动机，再指出文献忽视 CVC 与 licensing 的关系。gap 同时来自现实和文献。
- 文献综述动作：把 markets for technology 和 real options 两支文献合并成一张选择地图。MFT 提供 licensing/acquisition/alliance 菜单，RO 提供等待和行使逻辑。
- 方法呈现动作：用 two-stage sequential decision model 先画完整路径，再说明本文研究哪几条路径。这适合所有复杂战略选择论文。
- 结果解释动作：始终回到期权直觉。不确定性高提高等待价值；时间紧迫降低等待价值；价值高且不确定性低触发行使。
- 结论动作：强调不能孤立看一种外部 R&D 活动，而要合并理解策略组合；同时承认本文没有检验 post-execution performance。

## P1997：应用政策评估论文的完整但偏模板化写法

- 论文：Du, Feng, and Chen (2022), *Blue sky defense in low-carbon pilot cities*。
- 摘要动作：Based on panel data..., we employ..., regarding policy as a quasi-natural experiment..., results show..., mechanism analysis implies..., heterogeneity shows... 适合应用实证论文快速交代全套设计。
- 引言动作：先讲宏观政策背景，再指出已有研究的三个不足：指标粗、忽视空间外溢、机制不足。贡献段清楚但理论 tension 较弱。
- 方法写法：先构造结果变量，再进入 DID。这个顺序适合效率、生产率、风险指数、平台质量等研究。
- 结果组织：基准结果 -> 直接/间接/总效应 -> 替代变量 -> PSM -> event study -> placebo -> 机制 -> 异质性，是政策评估论文的完整流水线。
- 可改进写法：如果要更接近顶刊，需要更早制造“外溢方向不确定”的张力，例如污染转移和技术扩散谁占主导，而不是只说要考虑空间效应。

## P0994：战略实证论文的“旧解释 -> 新概念化 -> 可区分预测”写法

- 论文：Li, Chi, Lan, and Wang (2025), *Venture capital exit after venture IPO*。
- 标题动作：标题直接写行为对象 Venture capital exit after venture IPO，不提前把结论塞进标题。适合战略/创业实证论文，因为读者先关心“这个重要行为如何发生”。
- Abstract 动作：先交代旧理论 information economics 的解释，再提出本文 conceptualizes retained equity as an exchange option，随后给出 uncertainty 和 private information 的交互证据。摘要不是堆变量，而是按“旧解释不够 -> 新机制 -> 证据 -> 贡献”推进。
- Introduction 动作：先让读者接受 VC post-IPO exit 是重要管理行为，再指出 retained equity 不能只用 signal 解释。关键写法是把一个看似熟悉的现象重新命名为 option-like behavior，创造理论新鲜感。
- Theory 动作：不要说“我们还有另一个解释”就结束，而要推导出与旧解释不同的可检验含义。本文的好处是 exchange option 直接推出 uncertainty 与 private information 的交互，而不是只推出保留股权更多。
- Method 写法：把中国制度背景写成识别优势：mandatory lockup 让退出决策节点更清楚，手工季度持股追踪让退出行为更可观察。顶刊写法常把“数据麻烦”改写成“制度窗口”。
- Results 写法：非线性模型中的交互项不能只报系数，必须报告 marginal effects 和预测概率变化。本文把 uncertainty 从 25 分位到 75 分位带来的概率变化转成直观量级，这是实证写作里很值得学的动作。
- 语气动作：结论要写成 evidence is consistent with the exchange option view，而不是 prove。观察性战略论文越有现实含义，越要在因果语气上克制。
- 可复用表达功能：Existing explanations emphasize [old mechanism], but they do not fully account for [observed behavior under condition]. / We conceptualize [behavior] as [new theoretical object]. / This view yields a distinct prediction: the effect of [uncertainty] should be stronger when [private information] is more favorable. / The evidence is consistent with [mechanism], while not ruling out [alternative] entirely.

## P1491：中文管理实证的“selection -> signal interpretation -> boundary condition”写法

- 论文：陈晋等（2021），《企业创新信号与政府风险投资》。
- 标题动作：中文标题直接交代对象、机制和理论视角，信息完整。若顶刊英文改写，可加强 tension：When Innovation Signals Meet State Logic: Government Venture Capital and Patent-Based Selection in China。
- Abstract 动作：问题 -> 理论建构 -> 假设 -> 样本 -> 结果 -> 贡献。优点是完整；更顶刊化时应把“同一专利信号因接收者制度逻辑不同而变义”提前。
- Introduction 动作：先写 GVC 快速增长和政策重要性，再指出研究从 treatment 转向 selection。可复用写法是：Existing studies focus on post-investment effects, whereas we examine the antecedents of selection decisions.
- Literature Review 动作：把 GVC、signaling theory、institutional logics 三条文献线接成一个机制，而不是分别综述。中心句可以是：the meaning of a signal depends on the institutional logic of the audience interpreting it。
- Method 写法：先讲理论变量如何落地，再讲数据和回归。国有股权比例、本地投资占比不是随便找的变量，而是国家逻辑强弱的组织结构和行为约束代理。
- Results 写法：主效应回答“GVC 是否更偏好专利信号”；调节效应回答“国家逻辑何时更强”；早晚期轮次回答“专利是信号还是资源”；PSM 回答“是否只是资源禀赋差异”。每一步都服务一个审稿人会问的问题。
- 语气动作：这类非自然实验文章应写成 findings are consistent with the state-logic interpretation，而不是 prove。中文写作容易用“证实”，未来英文顶刊化要更克制。
- 可复用表达功能：We shift attention from the consequences of [actor] to its selection logic. / We argue that [signal] is interpreted differently by audiences embedded in different institutional logics. / We measure the strength of [logic] using [ownership/behavioral constraint]. / The results suggest that [logic] shapes not only whether [actor] selects [target], but also how it values [signal].

## P0152：JIBS 式“概念隐喻 + 双刃剑 tension + input/output 反差”写法

- 论文：Sun, Deng, and Wright (2021), *Partnering with Leviathan*。
- 标题动作：Partnering with Leviathan 用 Leviathan 暗示国家权力，副标题 The politics of innovation 明确机制。标题既有学术对象，又有概念张力。
- Abstract 动作：benefits and risks -> host state ownership -> innovation inputs and outputs -> contingencies -> contribution。最强记忆点是 despite opportunism, more R&D investment, but fewer innovation outputs。
- Introduction 动作：从 IJV 经典张力 value creation vs value appropriation 切入，再转入 foreign-host-state JVs。先挂到大理论问题，再进入中国情境。
- Theory 动作：不要把政府伙伴写成单一资源提供者。写成 resource provider + rent appropriator，既能帮助合作，也能制造压力。
- Method 写法：每个变量对应理论角色：R&D intensity 是 visible political commitment；patents 是 real innovation output；state ownership 是 political partner power；moderators 是 political pressure 的边界。
- Results 写法：系数要翻译成现实量级。state ownership 增加一个标准差对应约 696.3 万元 R&D 支出增加，比单纯报 p 值更有说服力。
- 可复用表达功能：Political partners can provide [resources], but their status also equips them to appropriate [rents]. / We distinguish between innovation inputs and innovation outputs. / The findings highlight both the powerfulness of [actor] to induce [visible investment] and its limits in shaping [real outcomes].
